Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Implications to our coverage: If next-gen AI server racks do not adopt PTFE-based
CCL, we think the outcome would skew positive for EMC and TUC but slightly

negative for Shengyi. We expect the material choice to be confirmed by mid-2027 at
the latest. Additionally, with EMC expecting its substrate CCL capacity to more than
double by end-2028, Unimicron and other substrate makers gain an alternative
supply source, which we view as positive. Substrate CCL carries higher gross
margins than PCB CCL, so a higher contribution from this business should improve
EMC’s profitability as S572T ramps from 4Q26. On ABF substrate, lead times of at

least two years for key equipment such as EPM’s ABF laminators imply that new
greenfield ABF substrate capacity can only ramp from 2029 onwards. This aligns
with our view that ABF substrate undersupply will deepen in 2027-28. Substrate
sizes going to 230mm x 230mm by 2028, implying only 4 substrates per panel, is
also supportive of our positive view on demand. This would make it very difficult to
control yield, as one area of defect on the panel would imply 25% yield loss.

PTFE CCL: Adoption of glass cloth-free PTFE CCLs in next-gen AI server systems
was the topic investors asked about most at the booths. As we have written
previously (here and here), pure PTFE CCLs could deliver the best signal integrity
(SI), making them the preferred solution for midplane/switch PCBs for some
customers. However, their processability at PCB makers remains a challenge for
now, particularly for high-layer-count boards. In addition, multilayer boards (MLBs)

using PTFE require special high-temperature presses capable of reaching close to
400°C, as standard vacuum presses are insufficient. In response, Taiwanese CCL
suppliers are also exploring other materials that could deliver M10-and-beyond
performance, including hydrocarbon (HC)-based, HC+PTFE hybrid and
benzocyclobutene (BCB)-based CCLs. We expect the CCL material for the next-gen
AI server rack to be confirmed by mid-2027 at the latest.
Other key takeaways can be found on the following page.

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
PTFE offers the best SI but is harder to process; TW CCL makers are less bullish
on PTFE and are also exploring HC, HC+PTFE hybrid & BCB.
Thin E-glass cloth shortages and price hikes to last through 1H27, at least, as
looms stay scarce and lines shift to LDK/T-glass.
EPM’s ABF laminators (95%+ global share) are a key bottleneck to ABF substrate

expansion, with 2+ year lead times and no new greenfield capacity until 2029.
Glass core substrate is likely not commercially viable in the next 2-3 years, with
crack inspection after ABF lamination currently being problematic.
October 26, 2026 01:42 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Substrate CCL: S572T, the most advanced substrate CCL on display at EMC’s booth,
enters mass production in 4Q26, with volumes expected to increase sequentially
throughout 2027. EMC expects its substrate CCL capacity to more than double from
current levels by end-2028 and said it will have multiple major ABF substrate
customers. It added that it has qualified additional low-CTE glass sources to ensure
sufficient supply.
Glass cloth shortage: Several CCL suppliers still cite thin E-glass cloth as the key

material bottleneck. They generally expect the shortage to persist through 1H27
with continued price hikes, and one supplier believes it could last through late 2027.
In their view, the tightness in electronic-grade glass cloth stems from insufficient
supply of Japanese-made looms, as well as from existing glass cloth suppliers either
prioritizing in-house demand or converting production lines to LDK1/2 and T-glass
cloth to meet strong AI demand. One glass cloth supplier noted it remains reluctant

to adopt non-Toyota looms given the higher defect rates and repair costs.
ABF laminator: We also visited the booth of Eternal Materials (1717.TW, Not
Covered), whose subsidiary Eternal Precision Mechanics (EPM, 7795.TW, Not
Covered) holds 95%+ global market share in vacuum laminators used to laminate
ABF onto substrate cores. EPM’s ABF laminators are now one of the key
bottlenecks in ABF substrate capacity expansion. Our checks with EPM suggest
equipment lead times have stretched to at least two years and EPM’s new

greenfield capacity will not come online until 2029; until then, EPM can only
debottleneck by optimizing existing capacity and leasing idle capacity from third
parties. For context, a new ABF substrate plant may require at least 10 sets of EPM’s
most advanced three-stage vacuum laminator and pre-track machine, depending on
factory layout.
Glass core substrate: Microcracks and the resulting package reliability issues are
currently the two key technical bottlenecks for glass core substrates. Existing

automated optical inspection (AOI) tools struggle to detect and identify microcracks
in the glass core, and laminating multiple layers of Ajinomoto Build-up Film (ABF)
onto it makes inspection impossible, according to Shinko. One equipment supplier
also flagged metallization of high-aspect-ratio through-glass vias (TGVs) as very
challenging. We understand this supplier is not involved in copper pillar mass
transfer, an alternative approach to TGV metallization.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

ab 27 October 2026
Global Research
First Read
Faraday Technology
New opportunities in advanced nodes
Q326 beat on turnkey strength and non-op gains; Q426 outlook positive
Q326 EPS of NT$1.07 came in above UBS/Street expectations mainly on  non-op gains.
Q326 sales came in at NT$3.66 bn (+11% QoQ), ahead of its original guidance but in
line with its comment  during Taiwan Summit with turnkey sales up 20% QoQ given
better support from OSAT and foundries, despite NRE declining 31% QoQ. Q326 GM

decreased 2.7ppt QoQ to 42.5% on higher turnkey mix, while  OPEX grew 9% QoQ  on
salary adjustment and higher bonus accrual. Non-op gain of NT$266mn mainly includes
valuation gains on an investee company. For Q426F, it guided sales  flat  QoQ remaining
at high levels with IP and NRE up QoQ, and turnkey down QoQ. GM is guided back to
~45%, reflecting normalized ASIC economics; OPEX should decline single digit QoQ.
2027 growth outlook supported by AI, advanced packaging and 12nm node

Management guided for double-digit sales growth in 2027, with all three businesses, IP ,
NRE and turnkey, expected to grow, driven by three key catalysts: AI, advanced
packaging and new UMC 12nm project contribution. For IP , growth should be
supported by higher royalties on foundry price hikes/customer capacity expansion and
initial  contribution from 12nm node; NRE should benefit from 2nm/4nm projects
entering peak recognition, with 3-4 ASIC tape-outs targeted in H127; while MP should

see growth in both MCU and ASIC as new projects gradually ramp. AI-related mix was at
8-9% of Q326 sales, and is expected to increase throughout 2027 alongside
accelerating advanced-packaging shipments. We expect 2027E sales to grow 37% YoY.
2nm design win opens new advanced-node and product opportunities
Faraday secured a 2nm GAA special purpose  ASIC project worth several tens of millions
of US dollars in NRE, which management described as its largest-ever design win and

most advanced project to date. The project spans front-end design and IP integration,
back-end design, customized 3D DRAM and advanced packaging, and marks Faraday's
first customized 3D DRAM and hybrid-bonding project. It expects to recognize its first
NRE milestone  in H127, followed by MP targeted within 1-2 years. Beyond 2nm, Faraday
is expanding into new applications including OCS, CXL and Si-cap, leveraging its
capabilities in advanced packaging, interposer design and ASIC integration. We believe

successful execution by Faraday and its foundry partner could strengthen Faraday's
positioning in advanced-node projects and create follow-on design-win opportunities.
Valuation: Raise PT to NT$200, stay Neutral
We raise    2026E EPS (reported) from NT$3.43 to NT$3.68 on better Q326, but lower
2027/28E EPS by 4%/1% to factor in softer CE demand and higher OPEX. We raise our
PT to NT$200 from NT$190 using 24x 2027E PE (vs prior 22x). We raise our multiple as

the recent 2nm project win should boost investor confidence, but stay Neutral as the
advanced nodes (4nm/2nm) turnkey revenue recognition depends on the foundry
partner's execution. We await clearer evidence before revisiting our assumptions.

Company Description
Faraday was founded in 1993, spun off from UMC's IP and semiconductor design service
department as the first IC design service company in Asia. It is an IC design and production
service provider that initially focused on specialty applications on mature nodes by partnering

with UMC and gradually upgraded to advanced nodes through working with Samsung. The
company had its IPO on the Taiwan Securities Exchange in 1999.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Faraday using PE methodology.
Downside risks include slower-than-expected advanced nodes migration, further delay of
advanced packaging ramp, and growing competition in specialty projects from China IC
design service companies.

### Page 4

Quantitative Research Review
UBS Global Research publishes a quantitative assessment of its analysts' responses to certain questions about the likelihood of an occurrence
of a number of short term factors in a product known as the 'Quantitative Research Review'.  The views for this month can be found below.
Views contained in this assessment on a particular stock reflect only the views on those short term factors which are a different timeframe to

the 12-month timeframe reflected in any equity rating set out in this note.  For previous responses please make reference to (i) previous UBS
Global Research reports; and (ii) where no applicable research report was published that month, the Quantitative Research Review which can
be found at https://neo.ubs.com/quantitative, or contact your UBS sales representative for access to the report or the Quantitative Research
Team on ubs-quant-answers@ubs.com. A consolidated report which contains all responses is also available and again you should contact your

UBS sales representative for details and pricing or the Quantitative Research Team on the email above.
Faraday Technology
Question Response
1. Is the industry structure facing the firm likely to improve or deteriorate over the
next six months? Rate on a scale of 1-5 (1 = getting worse, 3 = no change, 5 =
getting better, N/A = no view)
3
2. Is the regulatory/government environment facing the firm likely to improve or
deteriorate over the next six months? Rate on a scale of 1-5 (1 = getting tougher

3 = no change, 5 = getting better, N/A = no view)
3
3. Over the last 3-6 months in broad terms have things been improving/no
change/getting worse for this stock? Rate on a scale of 1-5 (1 = getting a lot
worse, 3 = not much change, 5 = getting a lot better, N/A = no view)
4

by the analyst to indicate that the stock's price target and/or rating may change in the near term, usually in response to an event that

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Hon Precision (7769.TW): Large package and CPO on track to drive 2027 
content growth; 3Q26 beat; TP up to NT$12,600; Buy
29 October 2026 | 3:16AM HKT
Hon Precision delivered better-than-expected 3Q26 results, and highlighted: 1) 
product mix upgrade: a higher ASP for its large-package (Mega Tray) solution, with 
20-30% of some customers’ new orders already on Mega Tray, 2) CPO Insertion 4 
customer qualification expected to finish by 2026 year-end, with mass production 
targeted in 1Q27, 3) a reiterated capacity expansion target of +50% for 2027, and 4) 
new projects expected to lift GM from 2H27. We remain positive on Hon Precision 
and raise our 12m TP to NT$12,600 (implied 49x 2027E P/E vs. 83% NI YoY in 
2027-28E on avg.). Maintain Buy. 
ATC platforms across FT and SLT to meet customers’ needs 
During the meeting, management laid out its ATC roadmap in detail, covering three 
platforms. ATC 3 uses liquid cooling and is adopted by an ASIC customer, with fast 
temperature ramp, c.10kW of power (vs. market mainstream of 2-3kW) and a 
25-125°C range. ATC 5 uses refrigerant and supports tri-temp (-70-125°C) at 
c.10kW, mainly for automotive customers. ATC 8 uses refrigerant with c.25kW of 
power, for high-power applications. For large packages such as 200mm x 200mm, 
management plans to handle 12kW by end-2026 and 25kW by end-2027. Overall, 
Hon offers a different ATC system for each customer’s test requirements and installs 
it on its FT or SLT handlers. Management believes this ability to meet varying 
temperature and power needs is why customers favor Hon’s products. 
Mega Tray solution across FT and SLT supports a higher ASP uplift 
The large-package (Mega Tray) solution targets end-customer packages larger than 
120mm x 150mm and is backward-compatible with current chip sizes. Small-volume 
shipments start in 2H26 and penetration is anticipated to rise in 2027. In final test 
(FT), Hon offers the HP9050, a dual-temp model, and the HP1050, a tri-temp model, 
which can be paired with different ATC platforms depending on customer needs. In 
system-level test (SLT), Hon offers the HP-3036, which supports 36 test boards in 
parallel (vs. 6-12 today), with an ASP more than 2x current, as longer test times make 
parallel testing increasingly important. Management emphasized the importance of 
integrating full automation and advanced AOI into these new handlers. The handlers 
can also be paired with Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs), which OSAT customers 
are actively adopting. On ASP , Mega Tray enjoys better price vs. the mainstream 
handler solution. For some customers, 20-30% of new orders are already on Mega 
Tray.

### Page 2

CPO qualification by 2026 year-end 
Management said its CPO Insertion 4 tool has been ready for mass production since 
end-October, with current shipments mainly small-volume engineering tools. Customer 
qualification starts in November and is expected to finish in December. The customer 
targets mass production in 1Q27, and Hon has prepared long lead time materials in 
advance. 
The tool tests simultaneously for optical and electrical performance. This requires two 
thermal systems, one for the ASIC and a separate system for the OEs, while ATE handles 
the electrical test. Hon’s in-house optical alignment stage is still under qualification. 
For CPC, Hon is focused on mechanical alignment. Some customers have strong demand 
and are looking to complete NPI as soon as possible. Hon is co-developing with a US 
networking customer and a major ATE vendor. 
2027 capacity expansion reiterated 
Looking into 2027, management reiterated its capacity expansion target of +50%. With 
order visibility for the next 2-3 quarters, we expect revenue to keep growing into 1H27. 
On margins, high-end ATC and Mega Tray shipments start in 4Q26, and CPO tools begin 
delivery in 1Q27. With a 3-6 month lag for equipment revenue recognition, management 
expects these new products to drive GM higher in 2H27. 
On competition, management said major OSATs currently mainly use Hon’s equipment, 
and challengers are mostly at an early stage. Management sees its advantage in ATC 
technology, where it leads peers, as well as a more complete product portfolio given 
widely varying customer requirements. On CPO, management sees its optical and 
electrical co-testing tool as next-generation equipment, leading in the market. 
Earnings revision and Valuation

3Q26 beat: Hon Precision’s 3Q26 revenue of NT$16.2bn (+17.4% QoQ, +97.5% YoY) 
came in 5.0% above GSe and 7.4% above Bloomberg consensus. Margin-wise, 3Q26 GM 
of 56.9% (vs. 56.1% in 2Q26) was in line with GSe/cons of 56.5%/57.0%. For opex, the 
company’s 3Q26 opex ratio of 7.3% was higher vs. GSe/cons of 5.6%/6.6%, primarily 
due to higher R&D expense. As a result, 3Q26 EPS of NT$37.33 came in 4.7% above GSe 
of NT$35.64 and 8.0% above cons of NT$34.61.

Earnings revision: We factor in 3Q26 results and raise our EPS by 2% / 2% / 1% in 
2026-28E mainly on higher revenues and GM, reflecting the better-than-expected 3Q26

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Hon Precision (7769.TW) 
Valuation: We are Buy rated on Hon Precision. Our 12-month TP of NT$12,600 is based 
on a target P/E of 32x (based on close peer correlation analysis) applied to our 2028E 
EPS, and discounted back to 2027E at 13.6% CoE. Our key assumptions for CoE include: 
(1) 1.5x beta, (2) a 4.25% risk-free rate, and (3) a market risk premium at 6.25%. 
Key downside risks to our views: (1) softer AI/HPC demand, (2) slower adoption of SLT 
among AI ASICs, and (3) intensifying competition.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

3481 群創光電(INNOLUX)

報告日期： 2026/10/27 研究員：曾智楷 (Kevin Tseng)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 2Q26 營收 637 億，毛利率 14.6% ，營利率 2.6% ，EPS 0.57
元。受惠於非顯示器比重持續提升 1H26 EPS 達 0.77 元。  
 公司今年處分三座廠房，累計獲利約為 200 億元，目前資產處
分已告一段落 ， 暫無進一步出售廠房計畫 ， 後續將配合FOPLP
投資調整既有廠區資源。  
 因上半年提前拉貨以及零組件漲價影響，消費型顯示器需求動
能相對保守；半導體事業部分， Chip-first 製程已進入量產，
每月出貨量約 4,000 萬片，而 RDL-first 與 TGV （玻璃基板）
製程部分，目前正與客戶緊密合作推進，預期玻璃基板封裝產
品將於 2028 年推出。

一、 2Q26 毛利率達 14.6% ，EPS 0.57 元 
群創 2Q26 營收 637 億，QoQ -4.6% ，YoY +13.1% ， 而獲利方面，
2Q26 營業毛利 92.7 億， QoQ -3.6% ，YoY +96.8% ，毛利率
14.6% ，QoQ +0.1ppts ，YoY +6.2ppts ，營業淨利 16.3 億，營利
率 2.6% ，2Q26 業外淨利 16.3 億，稅後淨利 46.5 億，單季稅後
EPS 0.57 元。而就上半年來看， 1H26 消費型顯示器佔整體營收
45% ，平均毛利率為 6%至 10% ；商用顯示器佔 13% ，平均毛利
率 16% 至 20%； 非顯示器領域佔42%， 平均毛利率11% 至 15% ，
受惠於商用、非顯示器比重提升， 1H26 EPS 達 0.77 元。

二、資產處分告一段落，後續聚焦 FOPLP 產能  
為充實營運資金、活化公司旗下資產，群創今年處分三座廠房， 3
月將南科模組廠以 8.8 億元售予南茂 (處分利益 6.59 億元 )，以及
將南科二廠以 63.25 億元售予矽品 (獲利約 58 億元 )。公司 4 月再
度宣布，將南科五廠房及附屬設施以總價 148.5 億元售予日月光
投控，預計處分利益高達 133 億元。累計今年群創認列的售廠總
獲利約為 197.59 億元。公司表示，目前資產處分已告一段落，暫
無進一步大規模出售廠房計畫 ， 後續將配合FOPLP 投資調整既有
廠區資源。

三. 消費性面板短期保守，持續朝高階封裝領域布局  
公司公告 9 月營收 199.95 億， MoM +6.9% ，YoY +0.7% ，3Q26
營收 575.8 億， QoQ -9.4% ，YoY -0.4% ，今年累計營收 1879.3
億， YoY +10.6% 。展望後市，在面板業務方面，受記憶體等零組
投資評等：區間操作 
目標價位：42.8~54.6 元 
     1.45x-1.85x27BVPS 
參考價位：46.75 元 
     1.58 X 2027 BVPS 
     2026/10/22 收盤

件漲價及筆電客戶提前於上半年拉貨影響，對消費性顯示器短期
動能相對保守，惟 50 吋以下 TV、高階電競與 OLED 顯示器，以
及商用顯示器支撐營運表現。而半導體事業部分，公司表示，目
前 Chip-first 製程已進入量產，每月出貨量突破 4,000 萬片，預計
今年與 2027 年將維持雙位數年增率 ， 至於RDL-first 與 TGV 製程
部分，由於 TGV 玻璃基板具備優異的散熱效率、低翹曲以及高密
度互連優勢，將瞄準 AI 與 HPC 晶片需求，目前正與全球策略夥
伴及客戶緊密合作推進 ， 預期玻璃基板封裝產品將於2028 年前推
出。預 估2026 全年營收 2451 億元 ，YoY +8.1% ， 毛利率14.2% ，
營利率 1.9%， 稅後淨利805 億 ， 全年稅後EPS 1.01 元。預 估2027
全年營收 2659 億元，YoY +8.5% ，毛利率 14.3% ，營利率 3.1% ，
稅後淨利 892 億，全年稅後 EPS 1.12 元，每股淨值 29.51 元。

四. 投資建議為 42.8~54.6 元區間操作  
公司持續投入半導體 FOPLP 領域，聚焦轉型、資產活化題材，但
考量到 4Q26 至明年上半年面板事業展望保守 ， 半導體事業部分，
後續仍須持續觀察客戶認證、量產進度及營運貢獻狀況。目前股
價交易於 2027 BVPS 約 1.64 倍 PBR 水準，以公司過往 PBR 介
於 0.4x~2.5x 區間水準，給予區間操作投資建議，目標價為
42.8~54.6 元(1.45x~1.85 x 2027 BVPS) 。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
群創光電 (INNOLUX) 為全球第三大面板廠，主要產品為大尺寸 (大
於 9 吋)與中小尺寸 (9 吋以下 )與延伸技術之薄膜電晶體液晶顯示
器(TFT-LCD) 相關產品，大尺寸產品應用於液晶電視、公共看板、
桌上型監視器、筆記型電腦與工業控制等；中小尺寸則為平板電
腦、可攜式影音播放器、車載、航空船舶、智能家居及手機、穿
戴裝置等應用，也可選擇性搭配各類型觸控面板；另針對特殊使
用需求，也提供應用於醫療、軍用、教育與電子紙應用之產品。
集團總部位於苗栗縣竹南鎮，台灣生產基地位於竹南、南科、台
南、高雄等地，另有海外生產基地則包含中國深圳、中國寧波、
中國南京、中國南海、荷蘭等地。 2025 年公司營收占比大宗消費
品(Commodity) 55% ；非大宗消費品 (Non-commodity) 16% ；非
顯示器領域群 (Non-display) 29% 。公司 NB 客戶為 DELL，液晶電
視客戶包括 Panasonic 、Sharp 、Sony、Toshiba 、Samsung 、TCL
等；手機及平板電腦客戶為 Samsung 、Amazon 、Apple 及華為；
車用面板客戶包括 BMW、福 特、General Motors 、 特斯拉等車廠。
國內主要競爭廠商為友達光電、瀚宇彩晶，國外廠商則包括

2. 群創連售三廠房，預估獲利近 200 億元 
為充實營運資金並挹注未來發展動能，群創近期積極活化旗下資
產。繼 3 月先後將南科模組廠以 8.8 億元售予南茂 (處分利益 6.59
億元 )，以及將南科二廠以 63.25 億元售予矽品 (獲利約 58 億元 )

4 
二.產業趨勢概況  
1. AI 需求推動，FOPLP 與玻璃基板市場 2030 年將突破 80 億美元 
根據  Counterpoint Research 報告，受惠於 AI 與高效能運算
(HPC) 強勁需求 ， 全球扇出型面板級封裝(FOPLP) 與玻璃基板市場
規模，預計將從 2024 年的 6.5 億美元飆升至 2030 年的 81 億美
元以上，其中 AI 與 HPC 應用將佔 FOPLP 總營收的 45.6% 。相較
於傳統有機基板，玻璃基板具備高互連密度、尺寸穩定與抗翹曲
等優勢，能支援次世代小晶片 (Chiplet) 架構與大型 AI 處理器，吸
引台積電、 Intel 、三星電機、日月光及力成等大廠積極擴展產能
與研發。區域佈局上，預計 2030 年台灣、日本與中國大陸將囊括
全球 84.8% 的面板級封裝產能。儘管目前仍面臨面板尺寸標準化
與玻璃穿孔 (TGV) 製程等量產挑戰，但在產業鏈合作推動下，整體
市場前景依舊十分樂觀。

圖三、 2024-2030 年各地區 FOPLP 面板產能預估

資料來源： Counterpoint

2. 零組件成本壓力與需求衰退交織，10 月面板價格全面持平 
根據 TrendForce 預估，進入第 4 季後，受上游零組件成本上漲壓
力遽增及終端採購需求衰退雙重影響， 10 月 TV、MNT 與 NB 三
大面板價格預估將全面持平。 TV 面板部分，雖有原廠調控產能並
喊漲，但主要品牌第 4 季採購需求季減約 5%，短期難接受漲價；
而 MNT 面板需求持續減弱，惟成本壓力使面板廠無讓步空間，買
賣雙方對持平具共識，後續將視 TV 面板能否成功漲價而定；另
外， NB 面板則因前六大品牌第 4 季採購需求恐季減近 30% ，出
貨動能嚴重萎縮，在成本高漲且降價無助爭取訂單的情況下，雙
方亦達成維持價格穩定的共識。

投控，預計處分利益高達 133 億元。累計今年群創認列的售廠總
獲利約為 197.59 億元。公司表示，目前資產處分已告一段落，暫
無進一步大規模出售廠房計畫 ， 後續將配合FOPLP 投資調整既有
廠區資源。

三. 消費性面板短期保守，持續朝高階封裝領域布局  
公司公告 9 月營收 199.95 億， MoM +6.9% ，YoY +0.7% ，3Q26
營收 575.8 億， QoQ -9.4% ，YoY -0.4% ，今年累計營收 1879.3
億， YoY +10.6% 。展望後市，在面板業務方面，受記憶體等零組
件漲價及筆電客戶提前於上半年拉貨影響，對消費性顯示器短期
動能相對保守，惟 50 吋以下 TV、高階電競與 OLED 顯示器，以
及商用顯示器支撐營運表現。而半導體事業部分，公司表示，目
前 Chip-first 製程已進入量產，每月出貨量突破 4,000 萬片，預計
今年與 2027 年將維持雙位數年增率 ， 至於RDL-first 與 TGV 製程
部分，由於 TGV 玻璃基板具備優異的散熱效率、低翹曲以及高密
度互連優勢，將瞄準 AI 與 HPC 晶片需求，目前正與全球策略夥
伴及客戶緊密合作推進 ， 預期玻璃基板封裝產品將於2028 年前推
出。預 估2026 全年營收 2451 億元 ，YoY +8.1% ， 毛利率14.2% ，
營利率 1.9%， 稅後淨利805 億 ， 全年稅後EPS 1.01 元。預 估2027

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113) 金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

晟田(4541)：航太及半導體客戶需求延續，帶動 2027 年獲利成長 
建議：中立轉買進

一、晟田為航太精密機械零件加工廠 
晟田成立於 1991 年 8 月，實收資本額 6.76 億元，截至
2Q26 底，員工約 686 位。主要從事航太零組件(包括發
動機機匣與懸塊、起落架)及機械零組件(包括半導體真
空泵浦零組件、食品飲料填充包裝設備零組件、工業自
動化精密傳動零組件等產品)之製造加工與銷售等業務。
2025 年前五大客戶為漢翔(2634)、日本 E 客戶、
SLS(Safran Landing Systems)、SPP (6355.JP)及長榮
航太(2645)。2026 年前 9 個月產品別營收佔比為航太零
組件(客供料) 48%，航太零組件(自購料) 25%，半導體
設備零組件 23%，其他 4%。

二 、 夏季電價及費用認列影響獲利 ， 預估3Q26 稅後 EPS 
1.26 元 
晟田 3Q26 營收 5.6 億元(+1.9%QoQ，+30.9%YoY)，
季增主因為受惠航太客供料客戶拉貨動能成長及半導體
客戶需求提升。獲利方面，儘管產品組合持續優化，惟
受到夏季電價及籌資相關費用認列影響，預估營業利益
1.01 億元(-6%QoQ，+97.1%YoY)。假設匯率持穩，預
估稅後淨利 0.85 億元(-1%QoQ，+34.4%YoY)， 稅後EPS 
1.26 元。 
三、產品組合持續優化，預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 1.3 元 
展望 4Q26，半導體方面，考量客戶需求持續強勁，有
望帶動出貨動能；航太方面，波音(BA.US) 737 系列機
型月產能有望於 2026 年底自 42 架提升至 47 架，另，
根據空中巴士(EADSY.US)規劃，2026 年預計交付 870
架，截至 Sep-26 交付達成率約 6 成，預期 4Q26 將加
速交機 ， 帶動航太零組件拉貨需求 。 整體而言 ， 預估4Q26
營收 5.62 億元(+0.5%QoQ，+20.7%YoY)。獲利方面，
隨產品組合持續優化，且無電價因素干擾，預估營業利
益 1.05 億元(+3.9%QoQ，+28.6%YoY)。 假設匯率持穩，
預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 1.3 元。 
四、需求延續，預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 4.51 元 
展望後市，儘管航太供應鏈仍受原物料短缺、人力及產
能瓶頸影響，惟波音及空巴在手訂單仍維持高位，顯示
終端需求具支撐。隨供應鏈瓶頸逐步緩解，預期將帶動
航太零組件拉貨需求回升，有利晟田營運成長。半導體
方面，受惠終端客戶持續擴大資本支出，帶動相關產品
需求穩定成長。整體而言，預估 2026 年營收 21.2 億元
(+29.1%YoY)。獲利方面，隨產品組合持續優化，預估
毛利率 25.1%，稅後淨利 3.1 億元(+122.9%YoY)，稅後
EPS 4.51 元。 
展望 2027 年，考量航空引擎零組件客戶需求強勁，晟
田規劃擴充相關產能，新廠有望於 2H27 投產；半導體
方面，持續優化相關產能，以因應客戶需求成長。整體
而言，預估 2027 年營收 23.5 億元(+10.7%YoY)。獲利
方面，考量新廠投產後折舊費用增加，預估毛利率年減
至 24.8%， 稅後淨利3.3 億元(+9%YoY)， 稅後EPS 4.58
元(以增資稀釋後股本計算)。 
五、中立轉買進理由 
評價方面，以 2027 年 EPS 估算，目前 PE 約為 13X，
介於晟田歷史區間 12X~20X 下緣 ， 調升至買進評等 ， 目
標價 75 元(2026F PE 17X)。

程曦資訊深耕客服委外市場 超過 30 年，具備長期公部門合作關
係、高客戶續約率及跨產業客服營運經驗，建立穩定營收基礎與市
場競爭優勢。隨 AI 技術逐步導入既有客服流程，有望降低人力成
本並提升營運效率，加上 AI 客服平台及軟體訂閱服務持續發展，
預期將帶動產品組合與 獲利結構改善。此外，公司取得 ISO/IEC 
42001 人工智慧管理系統認證，有助於提升高規格企業客戶的信
任度，進一步拓展民營企業市場。然而考量 AI 新事業仍處於發展
初期，短期研發費用投入較高，實際獲利貢獻仍需時間顯現，綜合
以上，研究部預估 程曦資訊 2027 年 營 收 為 13.99 億元，
Y+9.24%， 毛利率25.55%，Y+1.15ppts， 營業利益率15.22%，
Y+1.39ppts， 稅後純益1.93 億元 ，Y+16.34%，EPS 為 5.86 元，
參考同業本益比區間約 10~18 倍，給予 15 倍本益比評價，目標
價為 88 元，投資建議為買進。 
 程曦資訊(6898.TT)公司簡介 
程曦資訊(6898.TT)成立於 1993 年 8 月，於 2022 年 5 月登錄興櫃
戰略新板，同年 9 月轉至興櫃一般板，並於 2026 年 10 月 23 日正
式掛牌上櫃交易 。 公司為台灣專業企業流程委外(BPO)及客服營運服
務商，深耕客服市場逾 30 年，以「系統、流程、人力」一站式整合
服務為核心，結合 AI 技術與營運管理經驗，提供企業及政府機關完
整的客服委外解決方案。客戶結構涵蓋政府機關、國營事業、金融
保險、電子商務及國際品牌等，具備大型政府專案執行經驗與長期
客戶合作基礎。營運布 局方面，公司於台北、桃園、台中、台南、
高雄及屏東設有據點，並透過子公司程高資訊、點鑽整合行銷及印
尼程曦，分別拓展中南部客服市場、會員數據行銷及東南亞多語系
客服業務，持續擴大服務範疇與市場規模。

主要業務內容

置、維運與整體營運管理服務，範疇涵蓋人力招募調度、標準化

總處、勞動部、1999 專線） 、國營事業 （台灣高鐵、台電、
台水）及跨國企業品牌。 
1.2 客訴處理：處理高難度消費者爭議與客訴案例，協助企業
傾聽顧客需求並優化服務流程。 
1.3 主動外撥與行銷伴銷：運用進線銷售、追加銷售與交叉銷
售經驗 ， 協助客戶將客服中心由成本中心轉型為利潤中心 。 
1.4 全通路服務 ： 整合電話 、Webchat、LINE、FB Messenger、
IG 等多軌管道，提供文字談話、論壇管理與社群服務。 
1.5 技術諮詢客服：提供3C、APP 及電機產品之專業技術問題
諮詢 、 線上故障排除與報修支援 （如OPPO、Realme、Plaud 
AI 等客戶） 。

營運現況 
公部門與企業客服專案帶動成長，研發及人事費用增加壓抑獲利 
程曦資訊 1H26 營收為 6.22 億元，Y+11.28%，主要受惠公部門及
企業客戶客服委外需求持續成長，帶動常態性年約服務與任務型專
案收入增加。其中，常態性年約服務涵蓋移工服務、社會住宅及長
照等政府專案，提供穩定營收基礎；任務型專案則受惠普發現金客
服專案認列，加上企業 AI 客服及數位轉型需求增加，推升整體營運
規模。獲利方面，受惠高毛利任務型專案貢獻增加，以及人力調度

利益率表現。綜合以上，研究部預估程曦資訊2026 年營收為 12.80

AI 客服座艙提升營運效率，軟體訂閱服務推動獲利結構改善 
展望 2027 年，程曦資訊將持續推動 AI 客服座艙與全通路服務平台
整合，透過 AI IVR 、即時知識查詢、自動化通話摘要及智能質檢等
功能，降低客服人員重複性作業與教育訓練成本。隨相關應用逐步
導入既有客服專案，預期可降低營收成長對人力擴編的依賴，改善
傳統 BPO 業務的人力成本結構；另一方面，公司規劃將客服系統與
AI 應用模組轉化為雲端租賃服務，透過月費訂閱及功能加購增加軟
體收入來源， 公司目標 2027 年 AI 相關新業務營收占比 可望 達
5%~8%。考量初期收入規模仍有限，預期2027 年獲利改善主要來
自既有業務效率提升，隨後續訂閱客戶增加，長期有望進一步帶動
毛利率成長。

AI 治理認證強化競爭優勢，民營企業市場拓展擴大成長空間 
程曦資訊目前營收主要來自公部門， 2025 年公部門營收占比達
76%，未來將在既有公部門業務基礎上，積極拓展金融、能源、交
通及電商等民營企業市場，擴大跨產業客戶服務規模。10M26 公司
取得 ISO/IEC 420 01 人工智慧管理系統認證，結合既有 ISO/IEC 
27001 資訊安全管理能力，可望強化AI 應用在資料保護、風險管理
及法規遵循方面的可信賴度，提高承接大型企業 客服專案的競爭
力。另一方面，公司可透過既有客服營運經驗，進一步提供AI 流程
改造 、 數據分析及顧問服務 ， 增加單一客戶的服務項目與收入貢獻。
綜合以上，研究部預估程曦資訊 2027 年營收為 13.99 億元，
Y+9.24%，毛利率 25.55%，Y+1.15ppts，營業利益率 15.22%，
Y+1.39ppts，稅後純益 1.93 億元，Y+16.34%，EPS 為 5.86 元。

Document 215: 9月规模以上工业利润数据解读：9月规模以上工业利润总额同比明显提高

Source PDF: all report pdf/9月规模以上工业利润数据解读：9月规模以上工业利润总额同比明显提高.pdf
Report source: 国新证券股份
Date: 2026-11-01
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

市场研究部 2026年10月27日
事件
10 月 27 日，国家统计局发布，1-9 月，全国规模以上工业企
业实现利润总额 60337.4 亿元，同比增长 15.5%（按可比口径计
算）。
解读
一、1-9 月规上工业利润同比保持较高增长，9 月增速比 8 月
明显提高
1-9月，全国规模以上工业企业实现利润总额 60337.4 亿元，
同比增长 15.5%，比 1-8 月回落 0.2 个百分点，比 2025 年同期提
高 12.3个百分点，创 2022年以来同期同比增速新高。
其中，9月，规模以上工业企业利润总额同比增长 14.4%，比
8月提高 10.2个百分点，比 2025年 9月回落 7.2个百分点。
1-9 月，采矿业利润总额同比增长 38.2%，比 1-8 月提高 3.1
个百分点，制造业利润总额同比增长 17.0%，比 1-8月回落 0.4个
百分点，电力热力水的生产供应业同比-14.3%，降幅比 1-8月扩大
2.3个百分点。
9月，规模以上工业利润总额同比增速明显提高，主要受 PPI

上涨的影响。9月 PPI环比上涨 1.2%，比 8月提高 0.8个百分点，
同比上涨 5.0%，涨幅比 8 月提高 1.2 个百分点，环比同比涨幅均
扩大，当月利润改善明显。
二、1-9 月股份制企业利润较高增长，行业利润增速极端分化
1-9月，国有控股规模以上工业企业利润总额同比增长 13.1%，
比 1-8月提高 2.8个百分点；股份制企业利润总额同比增长 20.8%，
比 1-8月提高 0.4百分点；外商及港澳台商投资企业利润总额同比
增长 0.5%，比 1-8 月回落 1.8 个百分点；私营企业利润总额增长
8.7%，比 1-8月回落 1.7个百分点。
1-9 月，在 39 个可比工业大类行业中，17 个行业利润同比增
长，22个行业利润总额同比下降。
在利润总额超千亿的行业中，计算机、通信和其他电子设备制
造业利润同比增长 103.8%，有色金属冶炼和压延加工业利润同比
增长 79.7%，有色金属矿采选业利润总额同比增长 68.0%，化学原
宏观研究
9月规模以上工业利润总额同比明显提高

——9月规模以上工业利润数据解读
市场表现截至 2026.9.30

分析师：郝大明

证券研究报告

### Page 2

料和化学制品制造业利润同比增长 49.2%，煤炭开采和洗选业利润
同比增长 57.0%，石油和天然气开采业利润同比增长 21.3%。
1-9月，汽车制造业、酒饮料和精制茶制造业、电气机械和器
材制造业、电力热力生产和供应业利润总额同比分别-22.8%、-
14.3%、-5.9%、-17.9%。
三、重点产业链利润增速较高
电子相关行业利润高速增长。1-9 月，电子行业利润同比增长
1.0倍，比 1-8月回落 10个百分点。其中，电子专用材料制造、半
导体分立器件制造行业利润分别增长 2.2倍、51.7%；新一代信息
技术加速发展，带动光电子器件制造、电子电路制造行业利润分别
增长 69.8%、62.5%。
高技术制造业引领作用明显。1-9 月，规模以上高技术制造业
利润同比增长 52.0%，比 1-8 月回落 2.7个百分点。其中，光纤制
造、光缆制造行业利润分别增长 5.7倍和 1.1 倍；计算机外围设备
制造、计算机整机制造、工业控制计算机及系统制造行业利润分别
增长 2.8倍、1.7倍、90.9%。

原材料制造业利润快速增长。1-9 月，规模以上原材料制造业
利润同比增长 44.9%，比 1-8 月回落 2.4个百分点。其中，化学原
料和化学制品制造业利润增长 49.2%，比 1-8月回落 1.8个百分点；
有色金属冶炼和压延加工业利润同比增长 79.7%，比 1-8 月回落
3.2 个百分点；石油加工行业同比扭亏为盈，实现利润总额 842.2
亿元，比 1-8月增加 279.3亿元。
总体看，1-9月原材料制造业、高技术制造业、电子行业等行
业利润大幅增长，规模以上工业企业利润较高增长。9月 PPI环比、
同比涨幅扩大，工业利润增速明显提高。预测四季度工业利润同比
增速将比三季度小幅提高。
投资建议
1：1-9月规上原材料制造业利润总额同比较高增长。
2：1-9月规上新高技术制造业利润高增长。
3：1-9月规上电子相关行业利润高增长。
风险提示
1、地缘政治恶化超预期；2、欧美经济衰退超预期。

주가 추이

3Q26 매 출액 과 영 업 이익 은 비현 금 성 비용 항목 개 선 으로 시장 기대치
를 상 회 했 으나, 차량 판매 감소와 인 플 레 이션 등으로 연간 가이 던 스 는 
하 향 되 었 습니 다. 올해 생산 차질 에 따라 이 연된 신차 판 매를 ’27년에 
확대하며 매출/이익 회복을 가시 화 하 는 부분 에 주목 필 요 합니다. 
매출 성장은 금융부문이, 영업이익은 자동차부문이 기여 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 45.1조원(-3.5% 이하 Y oY), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%)을 기록하며 시장 기대치를 상회했다. 파업으로 인한 생산 차질
로 3Q 판매량 92.1만대(-11.3%)에 그치며 자동차부문 매출은 큰 폭으
로 감소(33조원, -10.0%)했으나, 금융부문 매출이 취급 자산 증가로 큰 
폭으로 성장(+27.8%)했다. 반면, 영업이익은 금리 상승에 따른 금융부문 
영업비용 확대 영향과 기타부문 실적 부진 발생과 달리 자동차부문은 전
년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소와 판매보증 충당부채 전입
액 감소 등 우호적 환율효과로 큰 폭의 증가세(+24.8%) 기록했다. 
’26년 가이던스 하향, 구조적 반등 요인은 아직 
3Q 실적 발표에서 주목할 부분은 가이던스 하향과 현금흐름이다. 연간 
판매목표를 연초 415.8만대에서 400만대로 하향했고 영업이익률 목표
는 6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 0.8%p 하향했으며, ’26년 투자계획은 
17.8조원에서 16조원으로 낮췄다. 특히, 투자계획 하향은 차량 판매 매
출 감소 대비 인센티브, 원자재 가격 상승 등으로 현금흐름이 악화된 
부분에 기인한 것으로 판단된다. 실적발표에서 전사적 원가 최적화 활
동 추진을 강조했으나, 이는 좀 더 장기적 측면에서 효과가 가시화될 
것으로 판단되며, 인플레이션 및 원화절상 상황 고려 시 구조적 반등은 
단기적으로 쉽지 않을 수 있다. 
절치부심의 2027년, 목표주가 600.000원 유지 
그러나 매크로 상황을 제 외 하 면  ’26년 하반기 생산 차질에 따른 신차 
판매효과가 ’27년 으 로 이연된 것 이 라 는 점에 주목 필요하다.   ‘북미 HEV, 유럽 
BEV’ 전략 하에서 엘란트라, 투싼 풀세인지, GV80, G80 등 고수익 HEV 차종 
중심 미국 수요 대응을 통해 Mix 개선하는 모습이 확인되고, 생산이 본 격 화 된 
아이오닉3 유럽 판매 확대 시 중국 로 컬 업 체 들 의 공세를 방 어 하 는 모 습 을 
확인하는 것이 가 능 할 것으로 판 단 한 다. 노 후 차 종 중심 운 영 에 따른 판매 둔화 
이외에 아직 근 원 적 판매 경쟁력 저하로는 판단하지 않는다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10월 30일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

### Page 1

삼성바이오로직스 (207940/KS) 
3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 
2026-10-06

제약/바이오. 이선경 / seonkyoung.lee@sks.co.kr / 3773-9089

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 2,000,000 원(유지) 
현재주가: 1,354,000 원 
상승여력: 47.7%

Signal: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 전망  
Key: 5 공장 매출 본격화, 미국 공장 매출 기여에 따른 성장 기대   
Step: 단기 불확실성보다는 중장기적 성장 방향성에 주목

3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 성장지속, 컨센 부합 전망   
26년 3분기 연결기준 매출액은 1조 3,741억원(+9.3%YoY, +4.0%QoQ), 영업이
익은 6,098억원(-3.7%YoY, +4.0%QoQ, OPM 44.4%)으로 컨센서스 매출액 1
조 3,450억원과 영업이익 6,063억원에 부합하는 실적을 전망한다.  
5월 파업 영향으로 약 20개 배치 생산분(약 1,500억원)에 대한 원가 손실이 인식되
고 3분기 평균 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락했음에도 불구하고, 3분기부터 매출로 
인식되는 5공장 PPQ 배치 및 미국 공장의 매출 기여로 지속적인 성장이 예상된다. 약 
1,500억원 규모의 생산 차질분은 고객사와 일정 조율을 통해 출하될 예정으로 이에 
따른 4분기 매출 성장을 기대해 볼 수 있다.   
신규 수주 회복과 모달리티 확장에 주목 
시장에서 우려했던 신규 수주는 관세 이슈로 고객사의 컨택이 감소하면서 올해 저조했
으나 4 월 Section 232 결과 발표 이후 논의가 회복 단계에 있어 연내 추가 신규 수주 
가능성은 열려 있다.  
항체 시장의 경쟁심화와 차세대 모달리티 확장에 대응하기 위해 결정한 폴리펩타이드
의 인수는 연내 마무리될 예정이다. 폴리펩타이드의 2028 년 목표 EBITDA 마진은 
25%로 동사 대비 낮은 상황이나, 펩타이드 기반 CDMO 시장 성장에 따른 마진 개선
도 가능하다. 현재 임단협, 환율, 미국 공장 및 폴리펩타이드 인수에 따른 회계 영향 등 
불확실성이 큰 상황이나, 수주 회복과 5 공장 매출 인식 본격화, 미국 공장 및 멀티 모
달리티 확장 등 중장기 성장 방향성에 주목할 필요가 있다.

작성자(이선경)는 본 조사분석자료에 게재된 내용들이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 신의성실하게 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다.

Document 271: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 
2026-10-14

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 820,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 366,500 원 
상승여력: 123.7%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 및 환율 영향으로 시장 예상치 하회 예상 
Key: 4Q26부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 계약 수주 모멘텀 기대 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 4 행정 엔진&FDC으로 주가 상승여력 
충분. 매수

3Q26 Preview: 컨센서스 하회하는 실적 기록 예상 
3Q26 연결 매출액 6조 464억원(+36.9% YoY, -4.5% QoQ), 영업이익 9,230억
원(+65.6% YoY, -11.2% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(1조 70억원)를 하회하는 실적 기
록할 것으로 추정한다. 이전 분기 대비 중대재해 및 하계휴가, 그리고 추석 연휴에 따른 
조업일수 감소 효과와 더불어 큰 폭의 환율 하락 영향(약 500억원) 때문이다. 홍범도
함 화재 및 임단협 관련 일회성 비용은 이번 분기가 아닌 4Q 에 반영될 것으로 예상한
다. 해양 부문에서 트리온 FPU 프로젝트 C/O에 따른 일부 환입 발생한 것으로 추정
되며, 해양 도크에서 컨테이너선 선체 블록 일부 건조로 고정비 감소효과 일부 상쇄 중
이다. 다만 올해 연내 혹은 내년 초 추가 해양 수주 없을 시 고정비 상승 우려 확대될 
전망이다. 동사 올해 상선 수주 목표(114.7억불)를 이미 초과하는 수주 실적 기록한 상
황이며, 고마진 선종인 LNGC 및 VLGC/VLAC 중심으로 수주했기 때문에 ‘28~’29
년 구간 경쟁사 대비 뚜렷한 선종 믹스 효과를 기대한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 82 만원으로 하향 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 97 만원에서 82
만원으로 하향한다. 중국의 공격적인 캐파 증설 지속과 더불어 중국 대형 조선사들의 
글로벌 선주가 발주한 LNGC 물량 및 원가 경쟁력 확대에 따른 상선 부문의 멀티플 
조정이 불가피하다는 판단이다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클
과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 FDC에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지
속되고 있는 중으로 파악된다. 올해 4 분기부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 부문의 본격적
인 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 전망이며, 해당 모멘텀에 따른 주가 상승
여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)를 유지한다.

작성자(한승한)는 본 조사분석자료에 게재된 내용들이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 신의성실하게 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다.

By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 31 Oct 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Monica Sauro / Unsplash

### Page 3

The S&P 500 fell 0.6% last week, and the Nasdaq dropped 2.0%, both
posting their third straight week of losses, as disappointing tech earnings
revived concerns about AI-related spending.
The FOMC held the federal funds rate (FFR) at 3.75%-4.00% on
Wednesday in a 10-2 vote, with the dissenters favoring a 25bps hike. We
believe the committee needs another 50bps of increases to get back to a
neutral FFR that is neither accommodative nor restrictive.

October’s employment report (Fri) caps a jam-packed week of labor
market data, including JOLTS (Tue), ADP (Wed), and Challenger and
jobless claims (Thu). ISM’s M-PMI (Mon) and NM-PMI (Wed) are also
due. Six Fed officials are scheduled to speak. Tuesday’s midterm
elections should be interesting. Rate decisions from the Reserve Bank of
Australia and the Bank of England and earnings reports from Apple,
Palantir, AMD, and Qualcomm round out the week's calendar. Here’s

more:
(1) Employment. October’s employment report is the economic headliner.
Payrolls rose 29,000 in September, lowering the three-month average to
50,700 (chart). The unemployment rate rose to 4.2% from 4.1%, while
average hourly earnings increased just 0.1% m/m. We expect a stronger
payroll gain than the consensus, of approximately 55,000.
Challenger’s October layoff announcements (Thu) follow September’s
count of 43,300. Technology led September’s announced cuts, at 10,800.

Initial unemployment claims (Thu) remain low, with a four-week average of

### Page 4

197,800 for the week ended October 24 (chart). We expect more of the
same for both labor market indicators this week.
The four-week moving average of ADP's employment measure peaked at
26,000 in late September before easing to 16,750 in early October (chart).
September’s monthly ADP report showed a 90,000 gain in private
payrolls, nearly double the Bureau of Labor Statistics’ 46,000.
September’s JOLTS report (Tue) follows August’s 7.1 million job openings,

down from 7.3 million in July. The share of consumers saying jobs are
“plentiful” was 26.7% in October, little changed from 27.0% in August
(chart).

### Page 5

(2) PMIs. ISM’s M-PMI (Mon) follows September’s 54.5 reading, with the
prices-paid index at 77.9. The NM-PMI (Wed) was 54.9 (chart). S&P
Global’s flash M-PMI edged up to 56.0 in October from September’s 55.9,
a 53-month high, while the flash NM-PMI eased to 57.9 from 58.8. Both
still point to solid ISM readings.
S&P 500 forward earnings rose 38.8% y/y as of October 26, up from
37.2% in September, suggesting more upside for the M-PMI (chart).

### Page 6

(3) Earnings. The earnings reporting season continues this week, with
31% of the S&P 500 companies set to report. Apple, Palantir, AMD, Arista
Networks, Qualcomm, and Arm headline the list.
Analysts’ consensus estimates imply Q3-2026 operating EPS growth for
S&P 500 companies of 23.8% y/y, down slightly from 24.1% last week,
after Alphabet, Amazon, and Intel missed estimates (chart). Q4 growth
estimates held at 30.2%.
(4) Global. The Reserve Bank of Australia (Tue) is expected to hike its

cash rate target by 25bps for a second straight meeting, to 4.85% from
4.60% (chart). The markets are pricing in 88% odds of that result, so it’s
no sure thing. Last week, the Reserve Bank of New Zealand held its
official cash rate at 2.75% despite 95% odds of a hike. Both central banks
are often seen as bellwethers for the global rate cycle.

### Page 7

The Bank of England (Thu) looks set to raise its rate by 25bps to 4.00%,
with 98% odds priced into the markets. The markets see a 35% chance of
another hike in December.
The Eurozone’s flash CPI (Wed) is expected to rise in October from
September’s 3.8% y/y, with the core rate holding at 2.5%. Among the
major economies, Spain’s headline rate was the highest at 5.0% in
September and Germany’s the lowest at 3.3% (chart).

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
TODAY’S INSIGHTS
Financial Markets
Markets were generally buoyed overnight, with Anthropic’s
IPO filing and Meta’s US$25bn equity offering closing five
times oversubscribed adding momentum back into the
AI trade, while softer oil prices provided further support.

Attention now turns to a series of key economic developments,
with Australian CPI due today alongside policy decisions and
accompanying guidance from the RBNZ, BoC and FOMC.
• US equities moved higher, supported by AI-related
stocks. The S&P 500 gained 0.8%, while the NASDAQ
outperformed, rising 1.2%, and the Dow Jones was
unchanged. European markets were mixed, with the Euro
Stoxx 50 up 0.8%, while the FTSE 100 fell 0.8% ahead of
the UK Budget.
• Tech-heavy Asian markets were buoyed as recent headlines

helped ease concerns around AI financing. The KOSPI led
gains, rising 3.1%, while the Nikkei 225 also performed
strongly, up 1.7%. The Hang Seng diverged from regional
peers, falling 0.7%. Locally, the ASX 200 gained 0.5%.
• US Treasury yields were little changed, with the 2-year
yield rising 1bp and the 10-year yield unchanged. Futures
markets remain cautious about pricing further Fed tightening
in 2026, with the probability of a hike at today’s FOMC

meeting sitting at just 7%, taking the implied year-end fed
funds rate to 4.10%.
• Australian government bond yields were little changed
ahead of today’s CPI release, with the 3-year yield rising
1bp and the 10-year yield falling 1bp. Swap markets remain
cautious around additional RBA tightening in 2026, with the
probability of a November rate hike priced at 62%, taking
the implied year-end cash rate to 4.80%.
• Pre-FOMC positioning supported the US dollar, with

the DXY up 0.3%. The AUD outperformed other major
currencies, rising 0.2% against the greenback to 0.7028 and
also gaining across the major crosses. The euro and yen both
fell 0.3% against the USD, while the sterling declined 0.2%.
• Oil prices declined on hopes that diplomacy could avert a
renewed escalation in the US-Iran conflict. Brent fell 1.6%
to US$114/bbl, while WTI declined 1.9% to US$99.9/bbl.
Dated Brent traded at US$121/bbl. Gold rose 1.1% ahead

of today’s FOMC meeting, while positive risk sentiment
supported base metals, with copper up 1.0% and aluminium
gaining 1.5%. Iron ore fell 1.1%, remaining below US$90/t
amid persistent concerns around global demand.
International Data
US durable goods orders rose 1.8% in September after a 0.1%
gain in August. Growth for core orders (non defence, ex aircraft)
was more modest in September at 0.5% but follows a 1.6% rise
in August.
US S&P Cotality Case Shiller house prices rose a further 0.3%

in August, accelerating annual growth at the margin to 2.7%yr.
US new home sales fell 0.6% in September following a
downwardly revised 5.1% gain (prev 6.4%) in August. Sales are
currently near the average of the past 5 years.
US Conference Board consumer confidence jumped from 84.2
to 91.7 in October on an improvement in current conditions
and expectations. While still below average, this measure of
consumer confidence continues to outperform the University of

Michigan survey, held up by the robust strength of the labour
market. Responses to the Michigan measure instead look to be
focused more on cost of living.
The Richmond Fed manufacturing index was unchanged at -2
in October.
Local Data
Our New Zealand team published their 2026 General Election
preview, outlining the major parties’ key policies (see here).

Document 405: Open mouth operations (2026-11-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Open mouth operations (2026-11-02).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 12
Status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #luciellis #rba
Open mouth operations
By Luci Ellis
Chief Economist Westpac Group, Westpac
13:34 October 23 2026 Share
The RBA does not want to promise what it will do, but it is increasingly trying to tell us what
it thinks.
Central banks’ communication is carefully scrutinised for every nuance. Lately, there seems to have
been more RBA communication to pore over. The method of delivery has also evolved. This has

### Page 2

Market participants pore over every utterance by central banks for clues to future rate moves. They will
have plenty to pore over in the run-up to the November RBA decision. After the flurry of fireside chats
this week, ’60 Minutes’ will run a pre-recorded interview with chief economist Sarah Hunter on Sunday.
Next Wednesday, the whole senior policy team will appear before Senate Estimates, just as the
quarterly CPI is released. (This timing will have been the Senate’s choice, not the RBA’s.) After the

decision will come the Statement on Monetary Policy, a media release and press conference. More
speeches will follow, including one by the Deputy Governor on the Thursday.
RBA communication does seem to have changed, not just following the RBA Review but also more
recently. There may be fewer policy meetings each year, but the communication outside that certainly
feels more frequent than a couple of years ago. Back in 2024, speaking engagements felt a little

sparse outside the post-meeting media conference and parliamentary appearances. We have counted
58 public engagements so far this year on the Speeches page on the RBA’s website. This compares
with 58 for the whole of last year and 52 in 2024. Excluding the ‘compulsory’ press conferences and
parliamentary appearances, fewer than half of the 2024 events were related to economics, markets
and monetary policy.
The ‘how’ has changed
The way the RBA communicates with markets has changed as well. There are fewer set-piece

speeches on policy and a lot more semi-scripted ‘fireside chats’. Compared with a few years ago, there
are more interviews across a wide range of media. This is a big change from the pre-Review world. It
was a huge deal when the previous Governor appeared on the 7:30 Report; it is hard to imagine him
agreeing to an Assistant Governor appearing on 60 Minutes.
Part of the change reflects increased efforts to reach a general audience. Central banks want to explain

why they do what they do. This matters especially when interest rates are rising but the household
sector is not the source of the inflation.
Speaking differently to different audiences can sometimes confuse markets. As the RBA’s Chief
Communication Officer highlighted in a recent panel discussion, the post-meeting media conference is
designed to speak to the public. Market participants and economists certainly tune in, but we are not
sometimes caused confusion when tone seems to vary. Different audiences call for different tone and

varying complexity.
The content has evolved, too. RBA officials are increasingly using speeches directed to professional
audiences to discuss more technical estimates of inferred variables such as neutral rates and
financial conditions. They are also highlighting that monetary policy combats inflation in more ways
than just squeezing mortgage holders.
One thing has not changed: the RBA is not in the business of providing ‘forward guidance’ promises

about the future path of interest rates. It does, however, give more running commentary about how it
is interpreting recent data. These interpretations are themselves useful signals about what the RBA
thinks it might do.

### Page 3

the primary audience. So if you think that what the Governor said in the media conference sounded
more ‘dovish’ than what another official says in a more market-oriented forum, it is partly because she
is trying to convey that the RBA understands that its recent policy actions are making things harder for
many Australians. But this is in much the same way that a doctor might say, “This might hurt a bit.” It
does, but that will not stop the treatment.

A related issue is that it is easier to control tone in pre-drafted material such as the minutes and set-
piece speeches than in unstructured Q&A. Senior central bankers are trained to be careful with their
words in unscripted settings. If the tone differs, the minutes are still more likely to say what the Board
really means than an off-the-cuff answer.
Greater communication with the broader public might have reduced the pressure to make the set-piece

speeches more accessible. Research on central bank communication often uses measures such as
the Flesch–Kincaid Grade Level and Flesch Reading Ease score. A comprehensive analysis was
beyond the scope of this note. However, a spot comparison suggests four speeches from 2026 (1, 2, 3,
4) were harder to read than four from 2021 (1, 2, 3, 4). The 2021 speeches scored in the 11 to 12
range, which is high-school-level complexity. The 2026 speeches scored between 12 and 15, which is

academic or technically dense text requiring university-level education. (Including two of my own 2021
speeches is an obvious caveat, but the two speeches by then-Governor Phil Lowe also scored better
on these metrics than the sample from this year. It should be noted, though, that readability metrics do
not necessarily imply better clarity if the concepts discussed are more abstract.)
The ‘what’ has changed
As noted above, the content of RBA communication has evolved, too. As its intellectual framework has

begun to put more weight on the balance of supply and demand, and those invisible ‘star’ variables
(NAIRUs, neutral real rates, ‘financial conditions’, etc.), the communication to professional audiences
has increasingly focused on those topics.
More recently, the RBA has also placed more emphasis on the full range of transmission channels from
interest rates to inflation (though not, as we have noted previously, the labour supply channel). This is

understandable in a world of supply shocks. People need to understand that monetary policy is not
simply an exercise in hitting mortgage borrowers. We doubt they would be comforted, though, by the
Deputy Governor’s explanation this week that the ‘cashflow channel’ was the smallest of the
transmission channels from interest rates to inflation, with the exchange rate much more prominent.
That implies a lot of pain for a small group, for little effect on inflation. It raises the question of whether

other policy tools or institutional arrangements might get the same effect on inflation through the other
channels without hitting a minority of households disproportionately.
We have also seen a move away from using catchphrases to summarise the position. From ‘narrow
path’ to ‘not ruling anything in or out’ to ‘cautious and predictable’, once entrenched, a phrase can
outlast its usefulness.

### Page 4

Related articles
One thing that has not changed is the way the minutes record the presentations to the Board and the
members’ discussion. The minutes rarely convey much new information beyond the post-meeting
statements. Sometimes, though, the minutes reveal the Board’s reaction to the staff’s analysis. The
August 2024 minutes were a good example: the discussion highlighted just how sensitive the forecasts
were to key judgements by the staff.
The ‘why’ has not changed

Despite these changes, the underlying objectives of the RBA’s communication have not changed. It
wants to explain – and perhaps justify – its decisions. And it wants to describe how it interprets
incoming data, especially inflation, the labour market and the balance of supply and demand.
The RBA, like any central bank, is not really in the business of providing ‘forward guidance’ about
future moves. Circumstances change, so it can never guarantee a particular future path for interest

rates. Some academic literature argues that promising to keep rates low for a long time (or high, for
that matter) independently amplifies the transmission of monetary policy beyond the effect of current-
period interest rates alone, by shifting the short end of the yield curve. Central banks including the RBA
have certainly tried jawboning in this way from time to time. The prevailing view, though, is that the
potential cost of reputational loss if things do not turn out as predicted outweighs the benefits of a

slightly stronger transmission mechanism.
In this context, it is the interpretation of data that provides the signal about future policy directions. This
is why it was noteworthy that the Governor still characterised the labour market as ‘a little tight’ after the
September meeting, even though the Board’s post-meeting communication did not. We have been
noting for some time that the RBA staff have a downbeat view of supply capacity. Together with the

RBA’s oft-stated concerns about inflation, this is why the Deputy Governor’s ‘hawkishness’ did not
surprise us.

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

People Bank of China (October 20)

We expect the PBOC to keep the 1Y LPR 
unchanged next week . Industrial 
production is expected to edge up from 
5.2% yoy in August to 5.3% in September, 
supported by firm external demand. 
Exports stayed strong, rising 36.3% yoy 
in September, driven by the regional AI -
electronics upcycle.

Domestic momentum, however, remains 
uneven. Retail sales growth is expected 
to moderate to 0.3% yoy  in September. 
Household sentiment is weak amid 
uncertain job prospects, slower income 
growth and elevated precautionary 
savings, while subdued property prices 
continue to weigh on household wealth 
and consumption. Investment also stays 
weak, with fixed as set investment 
expected to contract further by 7.5% yoy 
ytd in September. Corporate investment 
is cautious amid the anti -involution 
campaign and geopolitical tensions, 
while the property sector continues to be 
a major drag.

Policymakers are likely to maintain a 
measured policy stance, relying on 
targeted fiscal support to sustain growth 
rather than further rate cuts.

Bank Indonesia and Bangko Sentral ng 
Pilipinas (Oct 21 and 22).

Bank Indonesia is expected to extend its 
pause, as they lean on non-rate measures, 
including intervention and hedging 
facilities to help the currency.  On Friday, 
MSCI signaled a  potential shift in 
Indonesia’s status to a standalone 
status, rather than a reclassification to 
frontier market , after consultations are 
complete in mid -2027. Any ensuing risk -
off move in the currency , would likely 
prompt greater central bank vigilance 
and intervention.

Our base case for the benchmark rate to 
be held unchanged this quarter , backed 
by a lower conviction around back -to-
back US rate hikes in Oct and relative 
rupiah stability. Separately, the 
government announced additional 
support for low-income households in 4Q 
by way of in -kind assistance and wage 
subsidies. This year’s fiscal deficit i s 
likely to close in at -2.85% of GDP, close 
to our forecast at -2.9%, but few 
uncertainties facing the 2027 math (note).

We expect a 25bp hike from the BSP to 
5.25% in October , after three rate 
increases earlier in the year. Rising by the 
highest pace in Asia, Philippines inflation 
surged to 7.2% y/y in September from 6.1% 
in August, matching this year's peak and 
approaching a three -year high.  Inflation 
risks remain tilted to the upside, driven by 
further public transport fare adjustments, 
elevated oil prices, recent wage hikes 
and peso depreciation. Fiscal space is, 
meanwhile, limited to support 
households, via subsidies and/ instate 
price caps. Governor Remolona also 
signalled that the BSP remains ready to 
act on rates to contain inflation. With 
inflationary pressures likely to be 
persistent, the likelihood of further hikes 
in 4Q26 rises.

Bank of Korea (October 22)

The Bank of Korea is expected to hold its 
policy rate at 3.00%, following back -to-
back rate hikes in July and August. AI -
driven growth momentum remains strong, 
as reflected in September export growth 
of around 80% YoY. However, the

policy rate to 3.25% by year-end.

Japan

shortly. Export growth is expected to 
remain solid at 24% YoY, supported by 
continued strength in AI -related 
semiconductor exports. CPI inflation is 
projected to rise notably to 2.6% YoY,

Taiwan

September export orders and industrial 
production data are due shortly. Export 
orders are expected to sustain 60% YoY 
growth, while industrial production is 
projected to expand by 20% YoY. The 
global AI boom faces growing headwinds 
from hyperscalers’ cash flow pressures, 
central bank rate hikes, and higher bond 
yields. However, our high -frequency 
indicators across AI demand (token 
expenditure), investment (hyperscaler  
capex and data centre construction 
spending), and supply (semiconductor 
sales and exports) show no clear signs of 
a significant slowdown so far.

Malaysia

Malaysia’s external demand likely 
remained strong in September 2026, 
while inflation edged up slightly. We 
expect export growth to sustain its 
robust expansion at 45% yoy , with 
momentum anchored by strong demand 
for electrical and electronics goods. Oil 
& gas shipments likely remained buoyant. 
However, high base effects prevented a 
faster pace of expansion.

We expect headline inflation to rise 
modestly to 2.1% yoy in September 2026. 
The renewed increase in global oil prices 
likely contributed to higher increases in 
domestic energy costs, although fuel 
subsidies continued to limit the cost 
pass-through and keep underlying 
inflationary pressures contained. Food 
inflation likely also firmed, with adverse 
weather conditions associated with El 
Nino posing upside risks.

Document 483: “黑色星期五”的导火索：Anthropic只能“垃圾级”？

Source PDF: all report pdf/“黑色星期五”的导火索：Anthropic只能“垃圾级”？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-31
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

投资级评级能让 Anthropic 以更低利率借钱，为这些项⽬融资。⽽很多保险公司、养⽼基⾦
按规定只能投资级为主，或者限制持有垃圾级债务的规模。主体⼀旦掉到 BB+ 及以下，它
能触及的资⾦池将明显收窄，利率也会更⾼。
摘要：
⿊⽯告诉投资者， Anthropic 上市后可能⽆法⽴即获得投资级评级，预计主要评级机构将给予其
“ 较⾼档 ” 的垃圾级评级。
据英国《⾦融时报》援引知情⼈⼠报道称，⿊⽯向 Anthropic 最新⼀笔 630 亿美元芯⽚融资交易的
投资者传达了这⼀信息。若评级落在垃圾级， Anthropic 为逾 8000 亿美元的算⼒计划融资前景将
更加复杂。
美国财经博主、 YouTube 频道 Meet Kevin 主理⼈ Kevin Paffrath 认为，这则消息是周五美股加速
下跌的导⽕索： “ 消息⼀出，市场跌得更狠了。 ” 在他看来，症结在于 Anthropic 的算⼒承诺需要⼗
⼏年的利润才能覆盖，偏偏⼜拿不到投资级评级。
当天，亚⻢逊和英特尔的财报已加剧市场对 AI 热潮可持续性的担忧，美股在报道发布前就已⼤幅

⾛低；报道发布后，美国科技股进⼀步跌⾄⽇内低点，纳斯达克 100 指数收跌 3.5% 。
投资级预期落空
知情⼈⼠称，过去⼏个⽉，⿊⽯⼀直告诉投资伙伴，预计 Anthropic 将在年底前拿到投资级评
级。但根据主要评级机构给出的指引，⿊⽯近期改⼝称， Anthropic 获得 BB 区间或以上评级的可
能性更⼤。
按标普和惠誉的评级体系， BBB- 及以上为投资级， BB+ 及以下为投机级，市场俗称 “ 垃圾级 ” 。
BB 区间（ BB+ 、 BB 、 BB- ）是垃圾级中最⾼的⼀档，紧邻投资级⻔槛，这正是 “ 较⾼档 ” 垃圾级
的含义。
这⼀评级对 Anthropic ⾄关重要。公司周⼀提交上市申请，下⽉可能完成有史以来规模最⼤的
IPO 。招股书勾勒了⼀项 “ ⼤胆但理性 ” （ bold but sane ）的计划：从现在到 2045 年，在算⼒基础
设施上投⼊逾 8000 亿美元。
⽬前， Anthropic 持有逾 1120 亿美元现⾦及等价物，投资者预计 IPO 还将募资约 1000 亿美元。

投资级评级能让 Anthropic 以更低利率借钱，为这些项⽬融资。⽽很多保险公司、养⽼基⾦按规
定只能投资级为主，或者限制持有垃圾级债务的规模。主体⼀旦掉到 BB+ 及以下，它能触及的资
⾦池将明显收窄，利率也会更⾼。
“ 关键在于能否获得信贷，这⼀点⾮常重要 …… 如果（ Anthropic ）不是投资级，信贷成本就会上
升，这对谁都不是好事。 ” ⼀位专注美国科技股的基⾦经理说。对于当天的股市抛售，这位基⾦经
理的评价是： “ 外⾯简直是⼀场⾎洗。 ”
Anthropic 和竞争对⼿ OpenAI 的银⾏家都在游说⼏家最⼤的评级机构，希望两家 AI 公司上市后获
得投资级评级。对企业借贷成本影响更⼤的三⼤评级机构 —— 惠誉、穆迪和标普，⾄今尚未正
式给予 Anthropic 任何信⽤评级。
账⾯不差，承诺太重
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
在 Kevin Paffrath 看来， Anthropic 问题不在资产负债表。
据他解读， Anthropic 持有约 1120 亿美元现⾦和 190 亿美元短期投资，合计约 1310 亿美元，流动
负债仅约 70 亿美元；经营现⾦流已经转正，上半年⾃由现⾦流约 35 亿美元。若 IPO 再募资 1000
亿美元，可动⽤资⾦将达到约 2310 亿美元。
压⼒来⾃承诺的规模。Paffrath 测算，约 8000 亿美元的算⼒承诺扣除上述资⾦后，仍有约 5690
亿美元需要填补。假设 Anthropic 年底年化营收达到 800 亿美元、净利率为 40% ，每年约可贡献
320 亿美元，覆盖这⼀缺⼝需要约 17 年。
“ 相当于把 17 年的利润都押在了未来的算⼒承诺上，这才是让⼈紧张的地⽅。 ” 他说， “ 刚拿到垃圾

级评级，你要怎么做到？这就是今天让市场恼⽕的原因。 ”
Paffrath 还拿 SpaceX 作对⽐。他提到， SpaceX 已获得三⼤评级机构的投资级评级，原因可能
在于其业务不只依赖 AI ，还有⽕箭发射和星链。
不过， Paffrath 本⼈依然看多。他认为 Anthropic 的营收增⻓将超出市场预期；对于外界担忧的
供应商融资，他也不以为意，理由是博通净利率约 55% ，即便变相把芯⽚ “ 送 ” 给 Anthropic 也⾜
以覆盖成本。他将此轮下跌视为逢低买⼊的机会。
“ 北极星 ” ：借博通的信⽤融资
这笔 630 亿美元的融资代号 “ 北极星 ” （ Project North Star ），华尔街银⾏已于本周早些时候完成交
易。资⾦将⽤于 Anthropic 租赁⾕歌 1.5 吉⽡的芯⽚。⿊⽯担任锚定投资者，并与出资银⾏合作，
将债务转售给其他投资者。
交易结构显⽰出 Anthropic 对供应商信⽤的依赖。逾 420 亿美元优先级贷款由博通担保，博通参与
联合开发这批芯⽚，拥有 A 档⾼位的投资级评级，这是 Anthropic 能够拿到这笔融资的重要因素。

机构投资者⻘睐优先级债务，因为⼀旦违约，其受偿顺序排在次级贷款之前。
另有 180 亿美元次级债务以 Anthropic 的租赁付款为⽀撑，贷款⼈因此更直接地承担 Anthropic 的
信⽤⻛险。据《⾦融时报》此前报道，⿊⽯旗下基⾦以超过 10% 的⾼收益率认购了其中 90 亿美
元。
这部分次级债务获得 Kroll Bond Rating Agency （ KBRA ）给予的 BB+ 评级（仅⽐投资级低⼀个
⼦级），展望为正⾯，较四个⽉前类似交易 “ ⼤天空 ” （ Big Sky ）的稳定展望略有改善；晨星 DBRS
则给予 BB 评级。
Anthropic 、⿊⽯、 KBRA 、晨星 DBRS 和标普均拒绝置评，惠誉和穆迪未回复置评请求。

Document 486: 「関西の景気動向」９月号 AI関連需要の拡大と関西の製造業生産（PDF：841KB）

Source PDF: all report pdf/「関西の景気動向」９月号　AI関連需要の拡大と関西の製造業生産（PDF：841KB）.pdf
Report source: JRI
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

1

2026 年９月 
株式会社 日本総合研究所 
調査部 関西経済研究センター 
http://www.jri.co.jp/report/medium/kansai/

１．景気の現状と展望 
 関西の景気は、緩やかな回復基調を維持しつつも、一部に弱い動きがみられる。企業部門では、円安傾向に
加えて、世界的なAI関連需要の拡大を背景に輸出額は増加している。AI関連投資の活発化などを背景に、
鉱工業生産にも持ち直しの動きがみられる。設備投資は、中東情勢の混乱などによる先行き不透明感が意識
されるなかでも、企業の収益計画が高めの水準を維持するもとで拡大基調が続いている。一方、家計部門では、
所得環境の改善が続くものの物価高が重石となり、個人消費の回復ペースは緩慢にとどまっている。 
先行きを展望すると、中東情勢の混乱が長引くなか、資源価格の高止まりが企業活動の重石となる。一方、
世界的な AI 関連需要の拡大が関西の生産や輸出に波及する動きが続き、設備投資の押し上げにもつながる
と見込まれる。個人消費は、持続的な賃上げの動きが下支えに寄与するものの、物価高による下押し圧力が
根強く、回復ペースは引き続き緩慢にとどまる見通しである。

２．個別指標の動き 
（１） 輸出・生産動向 
関西の輸出額（円ベース）は大きく増加している。国・地域別にみると、中国（香港を含む）向けや、アジ
ア（除く中国・香港）向けの伸びが大きく、ともに半導体等電子部品の増加がけん引している。米国向けは振
れを伴いつつも増加傾向にある。品目別にみると、電気機器では半導体等電子部品、一般機械では半導体
等製造装置の増加が顕著にみられ、世界的な AI 関連需要の高まりが押し上げに寄与しているとみられる。金
属及び同製品も先端材料としての需要増や、それに伴う市況の高騰を背景に高水準で推移している。 
一方、輸出を数量面でみると、回復傾向にあるものの、ペースは緩やかにとどまっている。中国向けやアジア
NIEs 向けは低水準での推移が続いているほか、ASEAN 向けも伸び悩んでいる。米国向けは振れを伴いつつ
も、基調としては底堅く推移している。日本銀行が公表している実質輸出指数にも増勢の加速はみられない。

### Page 2

2 
以上のように、輸出数量の回復が緩慢にとどまる一方、輸出金額が大幅に増加しており、両者の動きに乖離
がみられる。これは、円安による輸出金額の押し上げや、AI 関連需要の拡大を受けた関連財の価格高騰によ
る影響が大きい。

鉱工業生産は、持ち直しの動きが続いている。業種別にみると、半導体製造装置やデータセンター向け設備
など AI 関連需要の裾野の広がりが追い風となっている生産用機械工業や電気・情報通信機械工業、機能性
素材や医薬品の増産を受けた化学工業の上振れが目立つ。一方、電子部品・デバイス工業は依然として低
水準での推移が続いている（関西における電子部品・デバイス生産の低迷について、詳しくは後述のトピックス
を参照）。

（２） 企業収益・設備投資動向 
企業収益を日本銀行大阪支店「短観」（６月調査、全規模・全産業）でみると、関西企業全体の 2026
年度の経常利益（全産業・全規模）は前年度比▲12.9％と、減益見通しとなった。中東情勢の混乱が長
引くなか資源価格の高止まりなどが収益の下押し圧力となっている。もっとも、2025 年度が同＋16.3％と、価
格転嫁の浸透などを背景に想定を大きく上回って着地したため、その反動減という側面も大きい。コロナ禍前と
の比較では、引き続き高めの水準を維持している。

### Page 3

3 
企業が高水準の収益を見込むもとで、関西企業は積極的な設備投資姿勢を維持している。日本銀行大
阪支店「短観」６月調査による2026年度の設備投資計画（全産業・全規模）は前年度比＋9.7％と、過
去（2010～2024 年度）の平均伸び率を上回る水準にある。機械設備や建設資材の価格上昇などから名
目の投資額が押し上げられている点は割り引く必要があるものの、中東情勢の緊迫化が企業の設備投資マイ
ンドに与える影響は今のところ限定的にとどまっている。ソフトウエア投資も前年度比＋5.1％と増勢が続いてお
り、効率化や省力化を目的とした企業の積極的な投資姿勢に変化はみられない。

（３） 雇用・所得動向 
関西の雇用環境は、人手不足を背景に良好な状態が続いている。雇用関連指標をみると、有効求人倍率
は１倍を超え、完全失業率（季節調整値）は 2％台後半の低位で推移している。雇用者数は高めの水準
を保っている。 
所得面をみると、府県別「毎月勤労統計調査」から推計した４～６月期の関西の一人当たり現金給与総
額は前年同期比＋4.3％と、前期に比べてプラス幅が拡大した。今年の春闘における高い賃上げ水準での妥
結や、夏季賞与の増加（大阪府内企業を対象とする調査では前年比＋2.5％）などを反映し、賃金は高め

の伸びとなった。雇用者報酬を実質ベースでみると、物
価高による下押し圧力の緩和も相まって、4～６月期
は前年同期比＋３％超の増加となった。もっとも、春
先以降の原油高の影響がラグを伴って波及すると予想
されるほか、中東情勢の混乱が長期化するなか、原油
価格は依然として高水準にある。先行き、物価の伸び
は再び拡大する公算が大きく、実質所得には下押し圧
力が強い状況が続く見込みである。

（４） 消費動向 
関西の小売販売額をみると、スーパー、コンビニエンスストア、百貨店の販売額は緩やかに増加している。 
需要側統計（家計調査）をみると、実質ベースで見た消費支出は、4 月に税負担の軽減が実施された自
動車購入の増加などによる一時的な押し上げがみられたものの、均してみれば、物価高への警戒感が根強いも
とで、回復の勢いは鈍い。 
消費者マインドは、中東情勢の混乱直後の大幅
な悪化から改善傾向にあるものの、依然として悪化
前の水準を回復していない。とりわけ、大幅な落ち込
みがみられた「暮らし向き」や「耐久消費財の買い時
判断」の改善が遅れている。地政学リスクの長期化を
背景とした物価高への根強い警戒感が消費者マイン
ドの重石となっている。 
先行きの個人消費は、賃上げの持続が下支えに
寄与するものの、消費者物価の伸びが再び拡大する
もとで、回復ペースは緩慢にとどまる公算が大きい。

（５）住宅・公共投資動向 
関西の新設住宅着工戸数は、2025 年
４月の法改正を受けて上下に大きく振れた
ものの、 その後は落ち着きを取り戻し、概ね
法改正の影響を受ける前の水準に復してい
る。住宅需要は、金利の上昇や建築費の高
騰などによる下押し圧力が強いものの、将来
の資産価値への期待や、金利・建築費の先
高観を背景とした需要の高まりが下支えとな
り、大幅な落ち込みには至っていない。

2026 年７月の公共工事請負金額は前
年同月比＋15.0％となった。国土強靱化や
インフラ更新需要のほか、建設コストの上昇な
どを背景に、概ね前年を上回るペースで推移
している。

（６）倒産件数 
関西の企業倒産件数は、振れを伴いながらも、均してみるとコロナ禍前を上回る水準で推移している。原材
料費の高騰や人件費の上昇などに加え、金利上昇局面における資金繰りの悪化が懸念される。金利の上昇
によって新たに赤字に陥る中小企業の割合は全国よりも関西の方が大きいとの試算もあり、倒産件数は高止ま
りする可能性が高い。

（７）観光動向 
関西の外国人延べ宿泊者数は、コロナ禍前を上回って推移している。中国人旅行者の落ち込みが続いてい
るものの、その他の国・地域からの旅行者が全体を下支えしている。もっとも、足元ではその他の国・地域からの
旅行者も伸びが鈍化しており、インバウンド需要は全体として頭打ち感が強い。日本人の延べ宿泊者数につい
ても、宿泊料の上昇などを背景に、足元でコロナ禍前の水準を割り込んでいる。

３．トピック：AI 関連需要の拡大と関西の製造業生産 
AI 関連需要が拡大するなか、関西の半導体製造装置の生産は、振れを伴いながらも均してみれば全国よ
り大きく増加している。加えて、AI 関連需要は非鉄金属やゴム製品など川中、川上分野の素材産業にも広く
波及している。こうした動きを背景に、関連産業では生産能力の増強を目的とした設備投資を活発化させる動
きがみられる。 
一方、関西の電子部品・デバイスの生産は低迷が続いており、持ち直しに転じているわが国全体の動きと乖
離が生じている。この背景には、①公的支援を受けた次世代半導体の大規模生産拠点が九州や東北、中部
などで本格稼働していること、②関西に本社を置く電子部品・デバイス企業であっても製造拠点が関西に集中
しているわけではなく、国内各地に分散していること、があげられる。

### Page 7

7 
ただし、関西の電子部品・デバイス企業は、高付加価値化の源泉となる研究開発機能を関西圏に集約して
いると推測される。実際、関西の電子部品・デバイス・電子回路製造業の就業者数は九州や中部と同水準な
がら、研究者・技術者の割合は関東に次いで高水準となっている。 
したがって、関西の電子部品・デバイス生産の低迷は、競争力の弱さを直ちに意味するものではなく、各社が
生産拠点と研究開発拠点の地域ごとの役割分担を踏まえて、最適な拠点配置を進めた結果と捉えるべきであ
ろう。

紀彥呈 / 資訊軟體、半導體  
+886-2-2326-9899#1940 / ianchi@cathayfut.com.tw

聯電 2303 TT  ( 電子–半導體 ) 
聯年攀升，價量齊揚 
投資與評價 
1. 聯電 3Q26 受惠成熟製程及 AI 相關需求回升，晶圓出貨季增 7.3%，稼
動率攀升至 92%，本業創近三年新高；4Q26 隨新加坡 Fab 12i 產能持
續開出，22nm 新產品及矽光子業務逐步貢獻，稼動率維持 90%以上。 
2. 聯電 26 年受惠成熟製程需求回升 、 新加坡Fab 12i 產能開出及稼動率提
升，EPS 7.33 元；27 年成熟製程供需趨緊，加上矽光子、TFLN、DTC
及先進封裝等高附加價值業務逐步貢獻 ，EPS 8.35 元， 營運創歷史新高。 
3. 目前聯電交易在 128 元，位於近三年參考本益比區間 9~22x 平均值位
置，考量成熟製程復甦加快，稼動率攀升，且先進封裝與矽光子將開始
貢獻營收，維持買進投資建議，因上調27 年獲利預估，本益比由平均值
之 16 倍調高至中間偏上之 18 倍，目標價由 130 元(16X 修正前 27(F) 
PER)，調升至 150 元(18X 修正後 27(F) PER)。 
重點更新 
成熟製程需求提升使聯電 3Q26 稼動率達 92%，本業創三近年新高 
聯電 3Q26 受惠於成熟製程供需結構持續改善，AI 相關 PMIC、通訊與消費
性電子需求回升 ， 整體產能利用率由2Q26 的 85%提升至 92%，但因漲價幅
度略低於原先預期，聯電 3Q26 營收 742.39 億元(+8.0% QoQ) ，略低於預
期。隨著產能利用率提升 ， 毛利率由前一季之32.5%提升至 34.0%(+1.5ppts 
QoQ)，營業利益 186.43 億元(+29.3% QoQ) ，惟 2Q26 因權益法認列較高
投資收益，3Q26 業外收支淨額由前一季之 302.36 億元降至 12.68 億元，稅
後淨利為 163.89 億元(-61.2% QoQ)，EPS 1.32 元，本業為三近年新高。 
預估聯電 4Q26 AI 需求支撐晶圓出貨成長，產能利用率維持九成以上 
隨著 AI 相關電源管理、FPGA 及高速連接晶片需求持續成長，加上成熟製程
供需逐步趨緊 ， 新加坡Fab 12i 產能持續擴充 ， 配合22nm 新產品逐步量產，
以及矽光子相關業務開始貢獻營收，但因 3Q26 產品價格調漲幅度低於預
期，故下修 4Q26 混合 ASP 預估，小幅下調聯電 4Q26 營收 1.5%至 777.49
億元(+4.7% QoQ)。儘管部分 PC 相關 LCD 控制 IC、DDIC 及 Wi-Fi 需求出
現放緩，但 AI 相關高附加價值產品占比提升，維持產能利用率 90%出頭之
預估，惟混合 ASP 調低影響毛利率表現，調降毛利率預估至 34.6%。下調稅
後淨利至 166.90 億元(+1.8% QoQ)，EPS 1.34 元。

### Page 2

報告內容 
成熟製程需求提升使聯電 3Q26 稼動率達 92%，本業創近三年新高 
聯電受惠於成熟製程供需結構持續改善，以及AI 相關電源管理、通訊與消費性電子需求回升，
3Q26 晶圓出貨量達 121.2 萬片 12 吋約當晶圓(+7.3% QoQ)， 創歷史新高 ， 整體產能利用率由
2Q26 的 85%提升至 92%，其中多座 12 吋晶圓廠已達滿載，8 吋產線亦隨客戶訂單回流而明
顯復甦 。 此外22/28nm 製程營收再創單季新高 ， 其中22nm 營收占晶圓銷售比重提升至 18.3%，
主要受惠於 DDIC、車用雷達及 Wi-Fi IC 等新產品持續導入，帶動晶圓出貨成長，本季針對非
長約客戶有進行部分漲價，推算平均漲價幅度約 5~6%，但由於 8 吋的產能利用率提升幅度大
於 12 吋，且 8 吋的 ASP 低於 12 吋，使得本季的混合 ASP 持平於前一季。 
因漲價幅度略低於原先預期，聯電 3Q26 營收 742.39 億元(+8.0% QoQ) ，略低於預期。隨著
產能利用率提升 ， 有效分攤晶圓製造固定成本 ， 且高附加價值22nm 及特殊製程產品持續增加，
抵銷部分折舊費用增加影響，毛利率由前一季之 32.5%提升至 34.0%(+1.5ppts QoQ)。在管理
及研發費用季減，以及其他營業收益增加帶動下，營業費用降至65.62 億元(-17.0% QoQ)，推
升營業利益至 186.43 億元(+29.3% QoQ)，營業利益率 25.1%。惟 2Q26 因權益法認列較高投
資收益 ， 使業外收支基期偏高 ，3Q26 營業外收支淨額由前一季之 302.36 億元降至 12.68 億元，
所得稅費用增加，稅後淨利為 163.89 億元(-61.2% QoQ)，EPS 1.32 元，本業為近三年新高。

預估聯電 4Q26 AI 需求支撐晶圓出貨成長，產能利用率維持九成以上 
隨著 AI 相關電源管理、FPGA 及高速連接晶片需求持續成長，加上成熟製程供需逐步趨緊，預
期聯電 4Q26 晶圓出貨量將維持低個位數季增 ， 美元計價ASP 亦保持穩定 。 此外新加坡Fab 12i
產能持續擴充 ， 帶動整體季度產能由132.5 萬片提升至 133.7 萬片 12 吋約當晶圓 ， 配合22nm
新產品逐步量產 ， 以及矽光子相關業務開始貢獻營收 ，但因 3Q26 產品價格調漲幅度低於預期，
故下修 4Q26 混合ASP 預估 ， 小幅下調聯電4Q26 營收預估1.5%至 777.49 億元(+4.7% QoQ)。

### Page 3

儘管部分 PC 相關 LCD 控制 IC、DDIC 及 Wi-Fi 需求出現放緩，但AI 相關高附加價值產品占比
提升，維持九成以上產能利用率之預估，惟混合 ASP 調低影響毛利率表現，調降毛利率預估
2.1 個百分點至 34.6%。下修營業利益預估 9.4%至 182.82 億元(-1.9% QoQ)， 營業利益率23.5%，
稅後淨利 166.90 億元(+1.8% QoQ)，EPS 1.34 元。

晶圓出貨與稼動率雙升，預期聯電 26 年營運創歷史新高 
聯電 26 年受惠於成熟製程供需環境改善，AI 相關需求持續帶動電源管理、FPGA 及高速連接
晶片成長，配合 22/28nm 製程在 DDIC、車用雷達及 Wi-Fi 等應用的新產品持續導入，相較
25 年產能利用率受消費性電子需求疲弱及成熟製程庫存調整影響，26 年自第二季起晶圓出貨
逐季回升，整體產能利用率由 1Q26 的 79%提升至 3Q26 的 92%，第四季亦預期維持 90%以
上，其中 22nm 製程營收占比持續提升，顯示高價值製程需求逐步增強。此外聯電積極布局矽
光子、TFLN 及先進封裝等新技術，26 年已與 SILITH 達成矽光子產品量產里程碑，並與國內其
他廠商合作開發 AI 伺服器應用 DTC 深溝槽電容，相關技術逐步由產品驗證轉向商業化。 
聯電新加坡 Fab 12i 產能也逐步開出，與 Intel 合作之 12nm 專案同樣進展順利，預計 26 年提
供 PDK 與 IP，應用涵蓋 Wi-Fi、數位電視與高速介面，惟推算 3Q26 產品價格調漲幅度低於原
先預期，小幅下修 26 年營收預估 1.2%至 2,817.58 億元(+18.6% YoY)。因 3Q26 混合 ASP 低
於預期及下調 4Q26 混合 ASP 預估 ， 小幅調低毛利率預估 1.3 個百分點至 32.7%(+3.7ppts YoY)。
調降營業利益預估 3.9%至 620.62 億元(+47.9% YoY)， 稅後淨利914.20 億元(+120.1% YoY)，
EPS 7.33 元，營運創歷史新高。

### Page 4

預期聯電 27 年 ASP 調漲與產品組合優化，推升營運再創新高 
預期聯電 27 年主要成長動能將由 26 年之出貨量與產能利用率提升逐步轉向 ASP 調整及高附
加價值產品組合改善，隨著 AI 基礎建設持續擴大，電源管理 IC、FPGA 及高速連接晶片需求維
持強勁，加上部分 8 吋及 12 吋製程已接近滿載，成熟製程供需結構持續趨緊，有利聯電與客
戶進行價格協商，預估 1Q27 混合 ASP 季增 7~8%。22nm 製程持續受惠於客戶新產品量產及
晶圓代工滲透率提升，矽光子則隨 12 吋 PIC 平台支援 1.6T 光通訊應用，以及 TFLN 高速調變
器與先進封裝整合技術逐步成熟，開始增加營收貢獻。雖然新增矽光子、DTC 及 TFLN 產能主
要於 28 年底後陸續開出 ， 使27 年晶圓出貨成長受到既有產能限制 ， 但預期透過製程瓶頸改善、
既有產能重新配置，以及高附加價值產品比重提升，仍可持續推升營運規模。 
上修 27 年營收預估 0.3%至 3,689.23 億元(+30.9% YoY)。在 ASP 改善、22nm 及特殊製程產
品組合優化，以及產能利用率維持 90%以上的帶動下，預期單位晶圓產值提高，且高稼動率有
助於分攤固定製造成本 ，上調毛利率預估 0.3 個百分點至 41.5%(+8.7ppts YoY)。 隨著矽光子、
TFLN、DTC 及 Hybrid Bonding 等技術持續開發，以及新加坡 P4 與台南 P7、P8 擴產計畫逐
步推進，相關研發、人力及管理費用將隨業務規模擴大而增加，故預估營業費用年增 37.9%至
416.00 億元，調升營業利益預估 1.0%至 1,113.59 億元(+79.4% YoY)，稅後淨利 1,038.76 億
元(+13.6% YoY)，EPS 8.35 元，營運再創歷史新高。

### Page 5

成熟製程需求改善及高價值特殊製程逐步放量，聯電稼動率維持高檔 
聯電短期受惠成熟製程供需結構改善，AI 相關電源管理、FPGA 及連接晶片需求持續成長，帶
動 8 吋與 12 吋產能利用率同步提升，3Q26 整體稼動率達 92%，部分製程已接近滿載。展望
27 年，隨產能供給趨緊及高附加價值產品占比提升，營運成長動能將由出貨量增加轉向 ASP
改善與產品組合優化，中長期則持續深化 22nm 特殊製程、矽光子、TFLN、DTC 及先進封裝
布局 ， 並透過新加坡與台南雙軌擴產計畫 ， 加速AI 相關產能建置 ， 其中12 吋 PIC 與 8 吋 TFLN
平台已支援 1.6T 高速光通訊應用，預計 27 年逐步增加營收貢獻，隨 28 年底後新增產能陸續
開出，進一步推升AI 相關業務占比，帶動公司由傳統成熟製程代工轉向高附加價值特殊製程及
異質整合平台。 
目前聯電股價交易在 128 元，位於近三年參考本益比區間 9~22x 平均值位置，考量成熟製程
復甦加快，稼動率攀升，且先進封裝與矽光子將開始貢獻營收，維持買進投資建議，因上調 27
年獲利預估，本益比由平均值之 16 倍調高至中間偏上之 18 倍，目標價由 130 元(16X 修正前
27(F) PER)調升至 150 元(18X 修正後 27(F) PER)。

### Page 6

公司簡介 
聯電是一家專注於純晶圓代工的半導體公司 ， 其核心競爭力並不在於先進製程節點的技術領先 ，
而是在成熟製程的量產能力、良率穩定度、成本控制與長期客戶關係。公司長期深耕 28 奈米
以上製程，並在顯示驅動 IC、影像感測、嵌入式非揮發記憶體、高壓與功率元件等特色製程平
台建立規模優勢。從產業定位來看，聯電更接近景氣循環型但具結構性支撐的公司，而非高度
技術競賽導向的成長型公司。

在獲利結構上，聯電的關鍵驅動因子主要來自稼動率與產品組合的變化。成熟製程的固定成本
比重高，因此當整體需求回溫、產能利用率上升時，毛利率通常會呈現明顯改善。相反地，若
需求疲弱導致稼動率下滑，即使營收跌幅有限，獲利仍可能受到放大衝擊。此外，營收成長的
品質也高度依賴產品組合，來自特色製程或車用、工控平台的訂單，對平均售價與毛利率的貢
獻，明顯優於一般消費性成熟製程產品。

成熟製程市場本身具有較強的商品化特性，價格與供需循環對聯電的短中期表現影響甚大。相
較先進製程由少數廠商主導，成熟製程更容易受到同業擴產、價格競爭以及客戶庫存調整的影
響。因此，在研究聯電時，不能僅以公司自身營運為出發點，而必須同時評估整體成熟製程產
能是否出現結構性過剩，以及終端市場是否進入補庫存或去庫存階段。

圖 2. Revenue by Application

資料來源: UMC、國泰證期研究部整理

在中長期結構上，車用與工業應用被視為聯電相對穩定的成長支柱。這類應用具備產品生命週
期長 、 轉換成本高與品質要求嚴格等特性 ， 有助於降低價格波動與需求急劇反轉的風險 。 然而，
其導入與認證週期較長，對短期財務數字的拉動效果有限，因此更適合作為評估聯電估值下緣
與營運穩定度的依據，而非短線成長動能。

### Page 7

聯電 ESG 政策 
聯華電子股份有限公司以創造以人為本、與環境共生、與社會共榮的全球性友善生態新價值作
為永續發展的核心願景，將環境(Environmental)、社會(Social)與公司治理(Governance)全面
整合至公司營運策略與決策架構之中 。 聯電認為 ， 企業長期競爭力不僅來自於技術與製造能力，
更奠基於穩健治理、負責任的環境管理，以及對員工、供應鏈與社會的正向影響。基於此，聯
電持續透過制度化的 ESG 政策與行動方案，確保公司在追求營運成長的同時，能有效降低對
環境與社會所造成的衝擊，並回應利害關係人之期待。

1. 公司治理： 
在公司治理面向，聯電以董事會作為  ESG 治理之最高層級，負責督導永續策略方向與重大決
策。董事會下設之永續發展暨提名委員會由全體獨立董事組成，專責監督  ESG 治理成效，而 
ESG 指導委員會則由共同總經理擔任主席，結合多位核心高階主管，負責永續策略目標的訂定
與執行。透過此一由上而下的治理體系，聯電確保永續議題能夠實質納入公司策略、資本配置
與績效管理之中，並定期向董事會報告執行成果，使  ESG 不僅停留於政策宣示，而是成為公
司治理的一部分。 
圖 3. ESG 治理組織架構圖

資料來源:UMC、國泰證期研究部整理

2. 風險治理與誠信經營： 
在風險治理與誠信經營方面，聯電將  ESG 議題全面納入企業風險管理架構，依循  COSO 企
業風險管理原則，系統性辨識、評估並管理營運過程中可能產生的治理、環境與社會風險。企
業風險管理委員會負責跨部門整合風險資訊，並與  ESG 指導委員會保持密切連結，確保氣候
變遷、供應鏈、人權、資訊安全等重大永續風險，能即時反映於公司決策與因應策略之中。此
一治理設計，使聯電能在高度變動的產業環境中，維持營運穩健與長期韌性。 
圖 4. 企業風險管理組織架構圖

資料來源:UMC、國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 8

3. 環境政策： 
在環境政策方面，聯電承諾以低碳轉型與資源效率為核心，朝 2050 年淨零排放目標邁進，並
積極響應 RE100 再生能源倡議。公司持續推動氣候行動，透過製程改善、設備升級與能源結
構轉型，有效降低溫室氣體排放。至 2024 年，聯電範疇一與範疇二溫室氣體排放量已較基準
年減量 31%， 顯示公司正穩健地行走於既定的減碳路徑上 。 同時 ， 聯電亦導入內部碳定價機制，
將每噸二氧化碳當量的成本納入投資與管理決策，藉此驅動更多具實質效益的減碳行動。

圖 5. 環境目標與績效表

資料來源:UMC、國泰證期研究部整理

4. 水資源管理： 
除氣候行動外，聯電亦重視水資源管理、廢棄物減量與生物多樣性保護。公司透過提升再生水
與海淡水使用比例，降低對天然水資源的依賴，同時推動廢棄物資源化與循環經濟模式，持續
減少單位產品的環境足跡。在污染防制方面，聯電針對空氣污染與水污染設有嚴格管理機制，
確保排放表現優於法規標準，並逐步將自然相關風險與機會納入長期環境管理規劃。

5. 社會政策 
在社會政策方面，聯電以人權保障與人才發展為核心，致力打造安全、健康、多元與共融的職
場環境。公司遵循國際人權準則與責任商業聯盟行為守則，確保員工在招聘、任用、升遷與薪
酬制度中受到公平對待，並透過完善的職業安全與健康管理體系，持續降低職災風險。在人才
培育與留任方面，聯電透過教育訓練、職涯發展與員工持股等制度，強化員工向心力與長期承
諾，使人力資本成為支持公司永續成長的重要基礎。

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 7 日

MURC Focus

スペインは解散総選挙へ

～右派連立政権が成立する見込み～

調査部 主任研究員 土田 陽介

1. 解散総選挙に臨んだサンチェス首相 
スペインのペドロ・サンチェス首相が 10 月 5 日、議会を解散し、11 月 29 日に総選挙（定数 350、比例代表制）
を行うと発表した。住宅価格や家賃の高騰に対する政府の緊急政令案が、10 月 2 日に下院で否決されたことを
受けて、少数与党による政権運営の行き詰まりを意識した首相が、国民に信を問う決断をした形である 。ただし、
サンチェス首相が率いる中道左派の与党・社会労働党（PSOE）の支持率は低迷している（図表 1）。

もともと PSOE の人気は低迷しており、前回 2023 年 7 月の総選挙でも、ライバル政党である中道右派の国民
党（PP）が第一党となっていた。しかしサンチェス首相は他の左派政党や地域政党の協力を受けて組閣し、政権
を維持した経緯がある。しかし首相周辺の汚職疑惑などから PSOE の人気は回復せず、次期の総選挙では PP
と同根の右派政党である Vox による連立政権が成立する公算が大きい。

〇スペインのサンチェス首相が10月5日、議会を解散し、11月29日に総選挙を行うと発表した。次期の総選挙
では野党第一党の PP と同根の右派政党 Vox による連立政権が成立する公算が大きい。 
〇PP と Vox が総選挙で勝利すれば、2018年6月以来の政権交代が実現する。こうした右派回帰を民族主義
の台頭という流れに位置付ける向きもあるが、スペインの国内事情はもっと複雑である。 
〇仮に総選挙を経て PP と Vox の連立政権が成立しても、両派間ですり合わせるべき課題も多く、スペイン政
治が安定するとは限らない。ゆえに、当面の問題への対応も進まないだろう。

### Page 2

解散の直接的な理由となった住宅価格の問題であるが、住宅市況が底にあった 2015 年を基準（＝100）とす
る指数でその動きを確認すると、直近2026 年4～6 月期は201 と、ユーロ圏全体（159）を大きく上回る（図表2）。
特に 2023 年以降の上昇が目覚ましいが、堅調な景気や移民の急増を受けて住宅需要が高まったことがその主
因である。

2. 民族主義的な右派回帰とは異なる 
PP と Vox が総選挙で勝利すれば、2018 年 6 月以来の政権交代が実現する。こうした右派回帰を民族主義
の台頭という流れに位置付ける向きもあるが、スペインの 国内事情は複雑であるため、必ずしもその流れに合わ
ない点、留意すべきである。まず、そもそも PSOE の人気そのものが低下している。確かに景気は堅調だが、住
宅価格の急騰のような社会問題に関して、サンチェス政権は具体的な処方箋を提示できなかった。

総選挙を経て政権交代が実現し、PP が Vox と連立を組んだとしても、それを民族主義の台頭の表れと理解す
るよりも、一定の期間ごとに繰り返される左派から右派へのリバランスの流れとして理解するほうが適切だろう。 ま
たスペインの場合、右派間に対立関係があることにも注目する必要がある。地域ナショナリズムが強いスペインで
は地方分権を求める保守の力が強く、中央集権を主張する保守（つまり Vox）と対立関係にある。

中央集権を望む保守の一部に戦前・戦後のフランコ体制を理想化する向きがあることも、地域ナショナリズムと
の対立を生んでいる。地域ナショナリズム政党の中には、自らを保守と位置付けながらも、中央集権を望む保守と
の対立から、PSOE や他の左派政党（Podemos や Sumar）と歩調を合わせる動きもある。スペインの民族主義は
多様なため、同国の右派回帰を反欧州連合（EU）や反移民の文脈で捉えることはできない。

例えばスペイン南東部のカタルーニャ州では、伝統的に左派も右派も民族主義を掲げ、独立運動（あるいは高
度な自治の獲得のための運動）を展開しているが、同時に票を奪い合う関係にある。最近は左派の カタルーニャ
共和主義左翼（ERC）が劣勢である。一方、右派のカタルーニャ同盟（AC）が優勢となっており、政党支持率では
PSOE に次ぐ第 2 位となっている。

AC は右派だが独立を主張しており、中央集権を主張する Vox とは対立する。一方、カタルーニャ州でも独立
に同調しない有権者は数多く、そうした有権者は PSOE に投票する傾向が強い。カタルーニャ州はスペインでも
有数の産業地帯であるため、労働運動が盛んだったことから、伝統的に PSOE の支持が厚いためである。このよ
うに、スペイン政治はまだら模様であるため、右派回帰と民族主義を同一視することは不適切である。

### Page 3

3. 総選挙後の新政権も多難な船出となる見込み 
総選挙後にいかなる立場の新政権が成立しようとも、スペイン経済の先行きに対する懸念は残る 。確かに景気
は堅調を維持しているが、一方、冒頭で述べた住宅価格の高騰のような社会問題に対する妙薬はなかなか存在
しない。加えて、経済成長の次なる牽引役を見いだせていないという問題もある。例えばスペイン景気を牽引する
輸出だが、旅行サービスに支えられたサービスの輸出は好調でもモノの輸出は不調である（図表3）。

中南米からの移民による労働供給が潤沢とはいえ、グローバルな労働力不足やスペインの人口構成（純増で
はあるが移民流入に依存した増加）に鑑みれば、スペインでも生産性の向上は不可欠である。それに資する総固
定資本形成は、実体として建設投資に偏っており、機械投資や知的財産投資の規模は限定的である（図表 4）。
これらの投資を増やすためには国による産業政策が欠かせない。

具体的には、規制緩和や税制優遇といった措置である。これまではサンチェス首相率いる PSOE 政権が少数
与党だったということや、PSOE が中道左派であるため経済成長よりも所得分配を重視したこともあり、そうした設
備投資を後押しするような政策が必ずしも打ち出されたわけではなかった。では PP と Vox の連立政権に交代し
たことでそれが可能になるかどうかというと、その可能性も高くない。

なぜならば、PP と Vox の連立政権が強いイニシアチブの下で産業政策を“上から”推し進めようとすると、中央
集権的な色彩が強くなり、地域ナショナリズムとの間であつれきが生じる展開が予想されるためである。中央集権
志向を弱めるという現実的な判断を Vox ができるかどうかが鍵を握るが、同党にはもともと PP から右派色の濃い
グループが離脱して結党した経緯があるだけに、その余地がどの程度か定かではない。

現状では総選挙を経て PSOE から PP と Vox の連立へと政権が交代する公算が大きいが、両党ですり合わせ
るべき課題も多く、政治が安定するとは限らない。ゆえに、当面の問題への対応も進まないだろう。 
図表 3. GDP に占める輸出の割合 図表 4. GDP に占める固定資本形成の割合

115.12 億元 (QoQ -5%、YoY+40%)，毛利率 49.36%，營益率 33.92%，
稅後淨利 28.47億元 (YoY+36%)，EPS 14.23 元。累計前三季營收 336.90
億元 (YoY+33%)，EPS 44.14 元。受惠中國自動化需求回升、生產效
率改善及規模經濟效益，營收與獲利同步成長， 惟 3Q26 有效稅率升

高。公司維持全年人民幣營收年增 20%以上、營益率 34%的指引。 
3Q26電子及工具機需求強勁，新產品成長動能延續 ：亞德客 3Q26電
子、電池、汽車及工具機營收占 比為 28%、14%、10%、8%，營收年
增 33%、25%、16%、35%，其中電子受惠 AI設備投資及智慧型手機
規格升級，預期 2027 年仍可維持雙位數成長；汽車業務市占率約
10%，仍具成長空間。傳統機械受惠中國十五五規劃推動智能製造及
設備更新，惟電池產業受擴產政策限制，短 期需求仍待觀察。 線性滑
軌 3Q26 營收年增 27%，稼動率由 2025 年 20%以上提升至目前 40%
以上，預計年底達 50%，毛利率可望提升至 30%，2027年營收預期成
長 20%以上。電控產品 2025年營收人民幣 4億元， 2026年至今成長
逾 20%，毛利率約 60%，年底前再推出三個新品系列； 半導體相關產
品月營收由人民幣 500 萬元增至 1,300 萬元，部分新品提前於 4Q26
上市，毛利率約 70%，2027年逐步貢獻營收。

Document 593: 何处是底

Date: 2026-11-01
